+import type { Session } from "./auth.ts";
+import { isReadToken } from "./read-token.ts";
+
+/** Only classified staging returns this exact bearer's current live lifetime.
+ * Matching the dispatch bearer is the caller's responsibility. Same-bearer
+ * preservation is monotonic, so a delayed old reply cannot undo a jump. */
+export function reconciledTiming(session: Session, response: Response): Session {
+  if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-timing") !== "staging") return session;
+  const expiry = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-expires"));
+  const capText = response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-cap");
+  const cap = capText === null ? undefined : Number(capText);
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expiry) || expiry <= 0) return session;
+  const read = isReadToken(session.token);
+  if (read && (cap === undefined || !Number.isSafeInteger(cap) || cap < expiry)) return session;
+  if (!read && cap !== undefined) return session;
+  const expiresAt = Math.max(session.expiresAt, expiry);
+  const capExpiresAt = cap === undefined ? undefined : Math.max(session.capExpiresAt ?? 0, cap);
+  if (expiresAt === session.expiresAt && capExpiresAt === session.capExpiresAt) return session;
+  return { ...session, expiresAt, ...(capExpiresAt === undefined ? {} : { capExpiresAt }) };
+}
+
+export function requestMatchesSession(request: { url: string; authorization: string | null }, base: string, session: Session): boolean {
+  return (request.url === base || request.url.startsWith(base + "/")) && request.authorization === `Bearer ${session.token}`;
+}
+
+/** Expired local metadata may be superseded by a preserving staging jump.
+ * Read-only probing creates no session and never widens or refreshes one. */
+export async function restoreStagingTiming(base: string, session: Session, fetcher: typeof fetch): Promise<Session | null> {
+  const aborter = new AbortController();
+  const timer = setTimeout(() => aborter.abort(), 10_000);
+  (timer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
+  try {
+    const response = await fetcher(`${base}/probe?scope=1`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${session.token}` }, signal: aborter.signal });
+    if (!response.ok) return null;
+    const next = reconciledTiming(session, response);
+    const stamp = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-time"));
+    if (next === session || !Number.isSafeInteger(stamp) || next.expiresAt <= stamp) return null;
+    // Consume the bounded ordinary probe body before blessing restoration.
+    const { readResponse } = await import("./http-response.ts");
+    const body = await readResponse(response);
+    return body.ok === true && body.viewer === session.principal && body.scope === (isReadToken(session.token) ? "read" : "full") ? next : null;
+  } catch { return null; }
+  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
+}
import type { Session } from "./auth.ts";
import { isReadToken } from "./read-token.ts";

/** Only classified staging returns this exact bearer's current live lifetime.
 * Matching the dispatch bearer is the caller's responsibility. Same-bearer
 * preservation is monotonic, so a delayed old reply cannot undo a jump. */
export function reconciledTiming(session: Session, response: Response): Session {
  if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-timing") !== "staging") return session;
  const expiry = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-expires"));
  const capText = response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-cap");
  const cap = capText === null ? undefined : Number(capText);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expiry) || expiry <= 0) return session;
  const read = isReadToken(session.token);
  if (read && (cap === undefined || !Number.isSafeInteger(cap) || cap < expiry)) return session;
  if (!read && cap !== undefined) return session;
  const expiresAt = Math.max(session.expiresAt, expiry);
  const capExpiresAt = cap === undefined ? undefined : Math.max(session.capExpiresAt ?? 0, cap);
  if (expiresAt === session.expiresAt && capExpiresAt === session.capExpiresAt) return session;
  return { ...session, expiresAt, ...(capExpiresAt === undefined ? {} : { capExpiresAt }) };
}

export function requestMatchesSession(request: { url: string; authorization: string | null }, base: string, session: Session): boolean {
  return (request.url === base || request.url.startsWith(base + "/")) && request.authorization === `Bearer ${session.token}`;
}

/** Expired local metadata may be superseded by a preserving staging jump.
 * Read-only probing creates no session and never widens or refreshes one. */
export async function restoreStagingTiming(base: string, session: Session, fetcher: typeof fetch): Promise<Session | null> {
  const aborter = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => aborter.abort(), 10_000);
  (timer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
  try {
    const response = await fetcher(`${base}/probe?scope=1`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${session.token}` }, signal: aborter.signal });
    if (!response.ok) return null;
    const next = reconciledTiming(session, response);
    const stamp = Number(response.headers.get("x-snapback-time"));
    if (next === session || !Number.isSafeInteger(stamp) || next.expiresAt <= stamp) return null;
    // Consume the bounded ordinary probe body before blessing restoration.
    const { readResponse } = await import("./http-response.ts");
    const body = await readResponse(response);
    return body.ok === true && body.viewer === session.principal && body.scope === (isReadToken(session.token) ? "read" : "full") ? next : null;
  } catch { return null; }
  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}

+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — reconcile the exact bearer before the SDK observes advanced business time.
+fn session_clock_headers(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Vec<Header> {
+    if !shared.staging { return clock_headers(shared).into(); }
+    let Ok(token) = crate::auth::bearer_token(request) else { return clock_headers(shared).into(); };
+    // Detached readers never fall back to the writer: callers may still hold it.
+    // Keep the process clock fixed across the committed authority snapshot, so
+    // preserved lifetime and the clock stamp describe the same staging jump.
+    let Ok((epoch, reader)) = shared.reader() else {
+        return vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Timing", "unavailable").unwrap()];
+    };
+    let identity = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
+    let clock = lock(&shared.clock);
+    let now = clock.now().max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire));
+    let timing = identity.as_deref().ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth("store identity unavailable".into()))
+        .and_then(|identity| reader.staging_session_timing(token, now, identity));
+    let headers = match timing {
+        Ok(timing) => {
+            let mut headers = vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Timing", "staging").unwrap(),
+                Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Time", now.to_string()).unwrap(),
+                Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Clock", clock.revision()).unwrap()];
+            if let Some(timing) = timing {
+                headers.push(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Expires", timing.expires_at.to_string()).unwrap());
+                if let Some(cap) = timing.cap_expires_at { headers.push(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Cap", cap.to_string()).unwrap()); }
+            }
+            headers
+        }
+        Err(_) => vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Session-Timing", "unavailable").unwrap()],
+    };
+    drop(clock);
+    lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
+    headers
+}

import type { Session } from "./contract.ts";

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#154-supplied-client-sessions — validate before effects and retain a private primitive snapshot.
export function suppliedSession(value: unknown, constructor: string): Session | undefined {
  if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  const invalid = (field: string): never => {
    throw new TypeError(`${constructor}: E_SESSION: session.${field} is missing or invalid. Pass the complete Session returned by guest(), login(), signup() or your service's session minting; one-line token-only fix: createClient({ url, token }).`);
  };
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) invalid("object");
  const input = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const principal = input.principal, kind = input.kind, token = input.token;
  const expiresAt = input.expiresAt, scope = input.scope, capExpiresAt = input.capExpiresAt;
  const fields: string[] = [];
  if (typeof principal !== "string" || principal.trim().length === 0) fields.push("principal");
  if (typeof kind !== "string" || kind.trim().length === 0) fields.push("kind");
  if (typeof token !== "string" || token.trim().length === 0) fields.push("token");
  if (typeof expiresAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(expiresAt) || expiresAt <= 0) fields.push("expiresAt (positive integer milliseconds)");
  if (scope !== undefined && scope !== "full" && scope !== "read") fields.push("scope (full or read)");
  if (capExpiresAt !== undefined && (typeof capExpiresAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(capExpiresAt) || capExpiresAt <= 0)) fields.push("capExpiresAt (positive integer milliseconds)");
  if (fields.length) invalid(fields.join(", session."));
  return Object.freeze({ principal, kind, token, expiresAt, ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(input, "scope") ? { scope } : {}), ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(input, "capExpiresAt") ? { capExpiresAt } : {}) }) as Session;
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — incomplete online credentials use the existing bearer-only path, without invented expiry.
/** Legacy online credentials carry only a bearer; identity and expiry stay server facts. */
export function suppliedOnlineSession(value: unknown): { session?: Session; token?: string } {
  if (value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
    const input = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    const token = input.token;
    if (!["principal", "kind", "token", "expiresAt"].every(key => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(input, key))
        && typeof token === "string" && token.trim().length > 0) {
      return { token };
    }
  }
  const session = suppliedSession(value, "createClient");
  return session ? { session } : {};
}
// Device time is an estimate, never an input to server authorization or settlement.
import type { DeviceCore } from "./local.ts";

const WINDOW_MS = 60_000;
const MAX_SAMPLES = 32;
const SHORT_RTT_MS = 1000;
const clocks = new WeakMap<typeof fetch, ServerClock>();
const readers = new WeakMap<() => number, ServerClock>();
const responses = new WeakMap<Response, { clock: ServerClock; order: number }>();
interface Sample { offset: number; roundTrip: number; received: number; provisional: boolean }

/** One bounded, minimum-round-trip window, reset on a server clock revision.
 * A clock advance must not compete with faster samples from before the jump. */
export class ServerClock {
  private offset = 0;
  private issuedOffset: number | undefined;
  private last = 0;
  private samples: Sample[] = [];
  private revision: string | undefined;
  private order = 0;
  private epochBarrier = 0;
  private listeners = new Set<(shift: number, reschedule: boolean) => void>();
  private responseListeners = new Set<(response: Response, request: { url: string; authorization: string | null }) => void>();
  private save: ((value: string) => Promise<void>) | undefined;
  private saving: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
  private lastSave: { owner: (value: string) => Promise<void>; value: string; result: Promise<void> } | undefined;
  constructor(private wall: () => number = Date.now) { readers.set(this.issuedDeviceNow, this); }
  fork(): ServerClock {
    const copy = new ServerClock(this.wall);
    copy.offset = this.offset; copy.issuedOffset = this.issuedOffset; copy.last = this.last;
    copy.samples = this.samples.slice(); copy.revision = this.revision;
    copy.order = this.order; copy.epochBarrier = this.epochBarrier;
    return copy;
  }
  /** Unfloored estimated server time: samples and server-answer refetches. */
  estimatedServerNow = (): number => Math.min(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, Math.max(0, Math.round(this.wall() + this.offset)));
  private deviceCandidate = (): number => Math.min(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, Math.max(0, Math.round(this.wall() + (this.issuedOffset ?? 0))));
  /** Monotonic issued device time: execution, prediction, expiry and rendering. */
  issuedDeviceNow = (): number => this.last = Math.max(this.last, this.deviceCandidate());
  /** Adopt a committed admission without changing the server-time estimate. */
  raiseIssued(time: number): void {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(time) || time < 0 || time <= this.last) return;
    const shift = time - Math.max(this.last, this.deviceCandidate());
    this.last = time;
    // Even a one-millisecond floor increase may cross a frozen deadline.
    if (shift > 0) for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(shift, true);
  }
  subscribe = (listener: (shift: number, reschedule: boolean) => void): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  };
  sample(serverNow: number, sent: number, received: number, revision?: string, order = ++this.order, provisional = false) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(serverNow) || serverNow < 0 || !Number.isFinite(sent) || !Number.isFinite(received) || received < sent) return;
    this.order = Math.max(this.order, order);
    const revisionChanged = revision !== undefined && revision !== this.revision;
    if (revisionChanged) {
      // A delayed reply from before an advance cannot restore the old window.
      const split = (value: string) => value.split(":");
      const [epoch, version] = split(revision), [priorEpoch, priorVersion] = split(this.revision ?? "");
      if (epoch === priorEpoch ? Number(version) < Number(priorVersion) : this.revision !== undefined && order <= this.epochBarrier) return;
      this.samples = [];
      this.revision = revision;
      // Every request already issued may still have reached the old process.
      if (epoch !== priorEpoch) this.epochBarrier = this.order;
    }
    const before = this.offset, issuedBefore = this.issuedOffset;
    const roundTrip = received - sent;
    // Long responses use a conservative receipt date, never a midpoint that
    // could latch half a server wait into the monotonic floor.
    provisional ||= roundTrip > SHORT_RTT_MS;
    this.samples = this.samples.filter(s => received >= s.received && received - s.received <= WINDOW_MS).slice(-(MAX_SAMPLES - 1));
    this.samples.push({ offset: serverNow - (provisional ? received : (sent + received) / 2), roundTrip, received, provisional });
    // Every short measured sample outranks provisional receipt stamps.
    const best = this.samples.reduce((best, s) => s.provisional !== best.provisional ? (best.provisional ? s : best) : s.roundTrip <= best.roundTrip ? s : best);
    this.offset = best.offset;
    this.issuedOffset = best.offset;
    if (before !== this.offset || issuedBefore !== this.issuedOffset) {
      void this.persist().catch(() => {}); // Device calls/close await the same durable save.
      // Watchers ignore sub-frame noise. A newly eligible issued offset can
      // move even when a short sample merely confirms the server estimate.
      const estimateShift = this.offset - before, issuedShift = (this.issuedOffset ?? 0) - (issuedBefore ?? 0);
      const shift = Math.abs(issuedShift) > Math.abs(estimateShift) ? issuedShift : estimateShift;
      for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(shift, revisionChanged);
    }
  }
  /** Credential metadata must be reconciled before a business-time sample
   * can reschedule expiry. The immutable dispatch bearer fences rotations. */
  beforeSample(listener: (response: Response, request: { url: string; authorization: string | null }) => void): () => void {
    this.responseListeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.responseListeners.delete(listener); };
  }
  wrap(fetcher: typeof fetch): typeof fetch {
    if (clocks.get(fetcher) === this) return fetcher;
    const wrapped: typeof fetch = async (input, init) => {
      const sent = this.wall(), order = ++this.order;
      const request = { url: typeof input === "string" ? input : input instanceof URL ? input.href : input.url,
        authorization: new Headers(init?.headers ?? (input instanceof Request ? input.headers : undefined)).get("authorization") };
      const response = await fetcher(input, init);
      const received = this.wall();
      // An unavailable staging authority snapshot cannot publish business time
      // without its credential lifetime. Stream-final samples obey the same rule.
      if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-session-timing") === "unavailable") return response;
      for (const listener of this.responseListeners) listener(response, request);
      responses.set(response, { clock: this, order });
      const stamp = response.headers.get("x-snapback-time");
      if (stamp !== null) this.sample(Number(stamp), sent, received, response.headers.get("x-snapback-clock") ?? undefined, order);
      return response;
    };
    clocks.set(wrapped, this);
    return wrapped;
  }
  serverDelay(deadline: number): number { return Math.max(0, deadline - this.estimatedServerNow()); }
  deviceDelay(deadline: number): number {
    return this.issuedDeviceNow() >= deadline ? 0 : Math.max(0, deadline - this.deviceCandidate());
  }
  sampleStream(serverNow: number, revision: string | undefined, order: number) {
    const received = this.wall();
    // The server writes this stamp at the end of its hold, not at request send.
    this.sample(serverNow, received, received, revision, order, true);
  }
  async attach(core: DeviceCore) {
    const kept = await core.call({ op: "meta", key: "server-clock" });
    if (kept.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(kept.denied));
    try {
      const state = JSON.parse(String(kept.ok)) as { offset: number; issuedOffset?: number } | null;
      const issuedOffset = state?.issuedOffset ?? state?.offset;
      if (state && Number.isFinite(state.offset) && Math.abs(state.offset) <= Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER && typeof issuedOffset === "number" && Number.isFinite(issuedOffset) && Math.abs(issuedOffset) <= Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) {
        if (!this.samples.length) this.offset = state.offset;
        this.issuedOffset ??= issuedOffset;
      }
    } catch { /* Missing or malformed clock metadata starts at offset zero. */ }
    // Only admissions restore a durable floor. Old offset metadata's `last`
    // may be a wall-time now() call and must not override a startup correction.
    // The device commits this floor atomically with admission, and retains it
    // after settlement. Pending entries also recover older stores/journals.
    // Backfill legacy admission times transactionally before replay can retire
    // the queue; offset persistence deliberately cannot retain an issued floor.
    const pending = await core.call({ op: "queued", restore_clock_floor: true });
    if (pending.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(pending.denied));
    const floor = await core.call({ op: "meta", key: "server-clock-floor" });
    if (floor.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(floor.denied));
    const issued = Number(floor.ok);
    if (Number.isSafeInteger(issued) && issued >= 0) this.last = Math.max(this.last, issued);
    for (const entry of (pending.ok ?? []) as { now?: number }[]) {
      if (Number.isSafeInteger(entry.now) && entry.now! >= 0) this.last = Math.max(this.last, entry.now!);
    }
    this.save = async value => {
      const reply = await core.call({ op: "set_meta", key: "server-clock", value });
      if (reply.denied) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(reply.denied));
    };
    await this.persist();
  }
  // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.3 — share unchanged offset persistence, never admission floors.
  persist(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.save) return Promise.resolve();
    const save = this.save, value = JSON.stringify({ offset: this.offset, issuedOffset: this.issuedOffset ?? 0 });
    if (this.lastSave?.owner === save && this.lastSave.value === value) return this.lastSave.result;
    this.saving = this.saving.catch(() => {}).then(() => save(value));
    const saved = this.lastSave = { owner: save, value, result: this.saving };
    // A failed save remains a failure to its callers, and is retried by the
    // next caller. Attaching another device always supplies a new owner.
    void this.saving.catch(() => { if (this.lastSave === saved) this.lastSave = undefined; });
    return this.saving;
  }
  async detach() { const pending = this.persist(); this.save = undefined; await pending; }
}

export const serverClock = (fetcher: typeof fetch): ServerClock => clocks.get(fetcher) ?? new ServerClock();

/** Streaming final stamps are provisional receipt-time samples, never poll midpoints. */
export function sampleChanges(response: Response, body: { serverNow?: number; clockRevision?: string }) {
  const sample = responses.get(response);
  if (sample && body.serverNow !== undefined) sample.clock.sampleStream(body.serverNow, body.clockRevision, sample.order);
}

export function onClock(now: () => number, listener: () => void): () => void {
  return readers.get(now)?.subscribe(listener) ?? (() => {});
}

export function clockDelay(now: () => number, deadline: number): number {
  return readers.get(now)?.deviceDelay(deadline) ?? Math.max(0, deadline - now());
}

  function stopForStore(code: "E_STORE_REPLACED" | "E_STORE_RESTORE" | "E_DEV_RESET") {
    if (storeRefusal) return;
    intentPresentation?.clear();
    void delivery?.reset(true);
    storeRefusal = { code, family: "store", site: "client", guide: "sync",
      message: `${code === "E_DEV_RESET" ? "reopen the client after /dev/reset" : code === "E_STORE_REPLACED" ? "the backend store identity changed" : "the backend store sequence regressed"}; outstanding writes may have executed and their outcomes are unknown; this client will not retry them`,
      rewrite: "close this client and create a new client for the current backend; reconcile outstanding writes before submitting new intent" };
    // @ref LLP 3000.023#3.4 — even lingering approvals withdraw before callbacks can inspect a peer.
    const deliver = deferReadNotifications(() => {
      endLingering(watches.values());
      queryEpoch++;
      // Terminal connectivity is observable without discarding the session that
      // createApp may validate on its replacement client.
      link = "signed-out";
      if (retry) { clearTimeout(retry); retry = undefined; }
      if (refreshTimer) clearTimeout(refreshTimer);
      aborter.abort();
      stopRefresh(storeRefusal!);
      for (const watch of watches.values()) { watch.reset({ denied: storeRefusal! }); scheduleExpiry(watch, null, null); }
    });

    // Retire every owner, including the request currently awaiting its body.
    // A late receipt must not settle it a second time or schedule another send.
    for (const entry of pending.splice(0)) {
      const outcome = { state: "failed", id: entry.id, why: storeRefusal } as const;
      entry.resolve(outcome);
      emitWrite(outcome);
    }
    for (const watch of watches.values()) notify(watch);
    for (const listener of linkListeners) listener(link);
    options.onStoreChange?.(storeRefusal);
  }

  function observeStore(json: Record<string, unknown>, floorAtDispatch: number, receipt = false, touched?: Changes): void {
    if (storeRefusal) return;
    const why = (json.denied ?? json.why) as Refusal | undefined;
    if (why?.code === "E_STORE_REPLACED" || why?.code === "E_DEV_RESET" || why?.code === "E_STORE_RESTORE") { stopForStore(why.code); return; }
    const id = json.store_id;
    if (typeof id !== "string" || !id) return; // Older servers have no identity envelope.
    if (storeIdentity !== undefined && id !== storeIdentity) { stopForStore("E_STORE_REPLACED"); return; }
    storeIdentity = id;
    if (typeof json.generation === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(json.generation) && json.generation > queryGeneration) {
      queryGeneration = json.generation; pendingEvents.notify();
    }
    if (dispatch.store !== id) { dispatch.store = id; dispatch.owner?.observe(id); }
    // Replayed receipts name a historical commit; their head is the current
    // store watermark. A buffered response may predate a later observed write,
    // so restoration compares with the floor at dispatch, not at delivery.
    const head = receipt && json.replayed === true ? json.head : json.head ?? json.seq;
    if (typeof head !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(head) || head < 0) return;
    if (head < floorAtDispatch) { stopForStore("E_STORE_RESTORE"); return; }
    if (head > minimumReadSeq) queryChanges.change(head, touched);
    minimumReadSeq = Math.max(minimumReadSeq, head);
  }

  const scheduleRefresh = (retryAfter?: number) => {
    if (refreshTimer) clearTimeout(refreshTimer);
    refreshTimer = undefined;
    if (closed || storeRefusal || !credentials || !session || session.kind === "action") return;
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — read sessions expire without any SDK rotation.
    if (readGate || isReadToken(session.token)) {
      const remaining = session.expiresAt - clock.issuedDeviceNow();
      if (remaining <= 0) { endCredentials(); return; }
      refreshTimer = setTimeout(() => scheduleRefresh(), Math.min(remaining, 2_147_000_000));
      (refreshTimer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
      return;
    }
    const untilDue = Math.max(0, session.expiresAt - clock.issuedDeviceNow() - refreshBeforeExpiry);
    const requested = retryAfter ?? (untilDue === 0 ? 60_000 : untilDue);
    const delay = Math.min(Math.max(1, requested), 2_147_000_000);
    refreshTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      refreshTimer = undefined;
      if (requested > delay) scheduleRefresh();
      else void refreshCurrent().catch(() => scheduleRefresh(60_000));
    }, delay);
    (refreshTimer as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
  };

  // @ref LLP 3000#7 — both client tiers keep short-lived sessions and rotate the bearer before expiry.
  function refreshCurrent(): Promise<SignIn> {
    const token = headers.authorization?.slice("Bearer ".length);
    if (readGate || isReadToken(token)) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, why: readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_REFRESH") });
    if (closed || storeRefusal) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, why: storeRefusal ?? ended() });
    if (refreshing) return refreshing;
    if (!credentials || !token || options.persona) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, why: authRefusal ?? { code: "E_AUTH", family: "auth", message: "sign in with a session to refresh it" } });
    const expectedPrincipal = viewer;
    credentialRevision += 1;
    const deliverReset = resetWatches(false, "credentials");
    const rotation = rotateSession(base, token, doFetch);
    let retired = false;
    const retire = (result: SignIn) => {
      if (retired || (!result.ok && (credentials || !result.why.retryable))) return;
      retired = true;
      // Only sign-out owns old-bearer cleanup after a transport rejection.
      // Store replacement keeps that bearer for createApp's recovery probe.
      void logout(base, result.ok ? result.session.token : token, doFetch);
    };
    // Never abort rotation: the server may already have consumed the old
    // token. Keep reading so a cancelled caller's successor can be revoked.
    void rotation.then(result => { if (closed || storeRefusal || !credentials) retire(result); });
    const work = (async (): Promise<SignIn> => {
      const refreshed = await Promise.race([rotation, refreshStopped]);
      if (storeRefusal) { retire(refreshed); return { ok: false, why: storeRefusal }; }
      if (closed) { retire(refreshed); return { ok: false, why: ended() }; }
      // Sign-out separately ends credentials and cancels adoption.
      if (!credentials) { retire(refreshed); return { ok: false, why: auth() }; }
      credentialRevision += 1;
      if (!refreshed.ok) {
        if (refreshed.why.code === "E_AUTH" && !refreshed.why.retryable) endCredentials(refreshed.why);
        else if (refreshed.why.retryable) scheduleRefresh(60_000);
        return refreshed;
      }
      if (expectedPrincipal !== null && refreshed.session.principal !== expectedPrincipal) {
        retire(refreshed);
        endCredentials();
        return { ok: false, why: { code: "E_AUTH", family: "auth", message: "a refreshed session changed principal" } };
      }
      session = refreshed.session;
      headers.authorization = `Bearer ${session.token}`;
      setViewer(session.principal as Principal);
      for (const listener of [...sessionListeners]) listener(session);
      if (!closed) {
        setLink("online");
        for (const watch of watches.values()) void scheduleRead(watch);
        scheduleRefresh();
      }
      return refreshed;
    })();
    refreshing = work;
    deliverReset();
    for (const watch of watches.values()) notify(watch);
    void work.finally(() => { if (refreshing === work) refreshing = null; }).catch(() => {});
    return work;
  }

  // A rotation invalidates the old token. Its late refusal is not a refusal
  // of the replacement: retry at the new revision (writes keep their id).
  async function obsoleteRefusal(why: Refusal | undefined, revision: number): Promise<boolean> {
    if (why?.code !== "E_AUTH" || why.retryable) return false;
    if (refreshing) await refreshing;
    if (storeRefusal || closed) return false;
    if (revision !== credentialRevision && credentials && !closed && !storeRefusal) return true;
import { refreshSession } from "./auth.ts";
import { isReadToken } from "./read-token.ts";
import type { Client, Refusal, Session } from "./contract.ts";
import { readResponse, transportRefusal } from "./http-response.ts";

export const readOnlyRefusal = (code = "E_READ_ONLY"): Refusal => ({ code, family: "auth", guide: "read-only",
  message: ({ E_READ_ONLY: "this read session permits reads only; use a full session for writes",
    E_READ_ONLY_REFRESH: "read sessions cannot refresh; sign in again or narrow a full session again",
    E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE: "a read session cannot open a device; use the online read-only client",
    E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL: "readOnly needs a server-verified read session, without a persona",
    E_READ_ONLY_UNSUPPORTED: "this server cannot verify read sessions; upgrade it before using readOnly" } as Record<string, string>)[code] ?? code });
export function refuseReadDevice(session?: Session | null, token?: string): void {
  // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.4 — token-based admission works offline and ignores forged scope fields.
  if (isReadToken(session?.token ?? token)) throw Object.assign(new Error("E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE: a read session cannot open a device"), { why: readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE") });
}
// Apply the same admission check to every device rotation, including a late
// successor. Refuse before adoption or persistence even if scope was forged.
export function refreshDeviceSession(...args: Parameters<typeof refreshSession>): ReturnType<typeof refreshSession> {
  return refreshSession(...args).then(result => { if (result.ok) refuseReadDevice(result.session); return result; });
}
export function validateReadOptions(options: { readOnly?: boolean; persona?: string; device?: unknown }): void {
  const code = options.readOnly && options.device ? "E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE" : options.readOnly && options.persona ? "E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL" : undefined;
  if (code) throw Object.assign(new Error(`${code}: ${readOnlyRefusal(code).message}`), { why: readOnlyRefusal(code) });
}

const readiness = new WeakMap<Client, () => Promise<Refusal | undefined>>();
export const registerReadVerification = (client: Client, verify: () => Promise<Refusal | undefined>) => { readiness.set(client, verify); };
export const verifyReadClient = (client: Client) => readiness.get(client)?.() ?? Promise.resolve(undefined);

// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.4 — nothing passes this gate, including
// exported asset credentials, before the exact bearer/revision/store is verified.
export class ReadScopeGate {
  private accepted: string | undefined;
  private pending: { key: string; work: Promise<Refusal | undefined> } | undefined;
  private failed: { key: string; why: Refusal } | undefined;
  constructor(private url: string, private transport: typeof fetch,
    private current: () => { token: string | undefined; revision: number; store: string | undefined; closed: boolean },
    private accept: (json: Record<string, unknown>) => void, private signal: AbortSignal) {}
  private key() { const c = this.current(); return JSON.stringify([c.token, c.revision, c.store]); }
  get verified() { return !this.current().closed && this.accepted === this.key(); }
  get refusal() { return this.failed?.key === this.key() ? this.failed.why : undefined; }
  verify(): Promise<Refusal | undefined> {
    if (this.verified) return Promise.resolve(undefined);
    const key = this.key();
    if (this.failed?.key === key) return Promise.resolve(this.failed.why);
    if (this.pending?.key === key) return this.pending.work;
    const c = this.current();
    this.accepted = undefined;
    const work = (async () => {
      let why: Refusal | undefined;
      try {
        if (c.closed || !c.token) return readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL");
        const response = await this.transport(`${this.url}/probe?scope=1`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${c.token}` }, signal: this.signal });
        const json = await readResponse(response);
        if (key !== this.key() || this.current().closed) return { code: "E_AUTH", family: "auth", message: "the read credential changed during verification" };
        if (json.denied) why = json.denied as Refusal;
        else if (!response.ok || json.ok !== true || !["full", "read"].includes(String(json.scope)) || typeof json.store_id !== "string") why = readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_UNSUPPORTED");
        else if (json.scope !== "read") why = readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL");
        else { this.accept(json); this.accepted = this.key(); }
      } catch (error) { why = transportRefusal(error); }
      if (why && key === this.key() && !why.retryable) this.failed = { key, why };
      return why;
    })();
    this.pending = { key, work };
    void work.finally(() => { if (this.pending?.work === work) this.pending = undefined; });
    return work;
  }
}

impl PollResponse<'_> {
    fn heartbeat(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
        let request = self.request.as_mut().unwrap();
        let mut frame = String::new();
        if !self.started {
            frame.push_str("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nCache-Control: no-store, no-transform\r\nX-Accel-Buffering: no\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n");
            // Clock time belongs to the final body, after the hold. A buffering
            // transport must not mistake early headers for an end stamp.
            for header in cors(request).into_iter().chain(request.connection_headers()).chain(super::ephemeral_headers(self.shared)).chain(super::latency::header()) { frame.push_str(&format!("{header}\r\n")); }
            frame.push_str("\r\n");
            self.started = true;
        }
        frame.push_str("1\r\n\n\r\n");
        request.write_response_frame(frame.as_bytes())
    }

    fn finish(mut self, status: u16, mut body: Json) {
        if !self.started { return respond_timed(self.request.take().unwrap(), self.shared, status, body); }
        // Stamp after the hold, including any development clock advance.
        let clock = lock(&self.shared.clock);
        body["serverNow"] = json!(clock.now().max(
                self.shared
                    .time_audience_clock
                    .load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire)
            )
        );
        body["clockRevision"] = json!(clock.revision());
        drop(clock);
        // Once heartbeats began the status is 200; the final JSON still
        // carries a typed denial, including a session revoked during the wait.
        let mut refused = status >= 400 || super::latency::Reply::refused(&body);
        let mut bytes = JsonBuffer(Vec::new());
        if serde_json::to_writer(&mut bytes, &body).is_err() {
            refused = true;
            let refusal = snapback4_core::error::Refusal::new("E_BOUND", "bound", "the HTTP JSON response exceeds 16 MiB; request fewer changes").guide("reads");
            bytes.0.clear();
            serde_json::to_writer(&mut bytes, &json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal), "serverNow": body["serverNow"], "clockRevision": body["clockRevision"] })).unwrap();
        }
        let mut frame = format!("{:x}\r\n", bytes.0.len()).into_bytes();
        frame.extend_from_slice(&bytes.0);
        frame.extend_from_slice(b"\r\n0\r\n\r\n");
        if self.request.as_mut().unwrap().write_response_frame(&frame).is_ok() {
            // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.7 — the final frame completes a held poll, not its first heartbeat.
            super::latency::finished(refused);
            self.request.take().unwrap().finish_stream();
        } else {
            super::latency::finished(true);
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for PollResponse<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // A failed or panicking stream cannot append a second response after
        // incomplete chunk framing, including on pipelined connections.
        if let Some(request) = self.request.take() { request.abort(); }
    }
}

pub(super) fn ack_preserved(engine: &snapback4_core::Engine, outcome: Option<&snapback4_core::MutationOutcome>) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
    match outcome {
        Some(out) if !out.replayed => match out.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent { .. } if out.receipt_only => {
                Some(snapback4_core::revocation::Footprint::default().preserved(engine.schema()))
            }
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent { seq, .. } => {
                engine.store().preserved_between(engine.schema(), seq.saturating_sub(1), seq)
            }
            _ => None,
        }, _ => None,
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.023#11 — certify the whole interval, including server-only inputs.
pub(super) fn settlement_changes(footprint: Option<snapback4_core::revocation::Footprint>, schema: &snapback4_core::Schema, after: u64, through: u64) -> Json {
    let Some(f) = footprint.filter(|f| !f.unknown) else { return Json::Null; };
    let mut public = snapback4_core::revocation::Footprint::default();
    for (name, c) in f.tables {
        if schema.visible(&name) {
            let target = public.tables.entry(name).or_default();
            target.deleted |= c.deleted; target.population |= c.population; target.columns.extend(c.columns);
        }
        else if let Some(owner) = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.target == name).find_map(|m| m.private.as_ref()) {
            // Private aggregate state changes its public owner field, never leaks its formula/table.
            public.tables.entry(owner.table.clone()).or_default().columns.insert(owner.column.clone());
        } else { return Json::Null; }
    }
    let writes: serde_json::Map<String, Json> = public.tables.into_iter().map(|(name, c)| {
            (name, if c.population || c.deleted { Json::Null } else { json!(c.columns) },
            )
        }).collect();
    json!({"after":after,"through":through,"writes":writes})
}
        promote_aged(&shared, Instant::now());
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
    }
}

fn promote_aged(shared: &Shared, now: Instant) {
    if lock(&shared.maintenance).eligible(now, true) { bulk_slice(shared, true); }
}

pub(super) fn bulk(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
    loop {
        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
        let observed = *lock(&shared.seq);
        let mut did_work = false;
        for _ in 0..3 {
            if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
            did_work |= bulk_slice(&shared, false);
            let pause = lock(&shared.maintenance).resume_at.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
            if !pause.is_zero() {
                // No shared lock survives this pause. Request commits do not
                // cancel repayment; empty kinds do not idle an active cycle.
                std::thread::sleep(pause.min(Duration::from_millis(100)));
            }
        }
        if !did_work {
            loop {
                let pause = lock(&shared.maintenance).empty_cycle(Instant::now());
                if pause.is_zero() { break; }
                std::thread::sleep(pause.min(Duration::from_millis(100)));
            }
            let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
            if *seq == observed {
                drop(shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, Duration::from_millis(100)).unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()));
            }
        }
    }
}

// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
        state.advancing = false;
        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
    }
}

fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if stopping {
        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
    }
    Ok(work)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — staging grants clock control, never fixture or persona authority.
pub(super) fn staging(shared: &Shared) -> bool {
    lock(&shared.engine).store().deployment_environment().ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging")
}
fn clock_service(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<Option<String>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if shared.personas { return Ok(None); }
    if !staging(shared) { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
    match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
        Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Ok(Some(principal)),
        _ => Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "staging clock control requires a full service token").guide("clock")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
        Ok(service) => service,
        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
    };
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let boolean = |key: &str, default: bool| -> Result<bool, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
        match payload.get(key) {
            None => Ok(default), Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => Ok(*value),
            Some(_) => Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("{key} must be a boolean"))),
        }
    };
    let (drain, allow_held) = match boolean("drain", true).and_then(|drain| Ok((drain, boolean("allowHeld", false)?))) {
        Ok(options) => options,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    if each_boundary && !drain { return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary requires drain:true"))})); }
    let preserve_sessions = match payload.get("sessions").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        None if payload.get("sessions").is_none() => !shared.personas,
        Some("preserve") if !shared.personas => true,
        Some("expire") => false,
        _ => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("sessions must be preserve (staging only) or expire"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(|duration| if duration == "0ms" && drain { Ok(0) } else { crate::dev_clock::duration(duration) }) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        if let Some(principal) = &service {
            if engine.store().deployment_environment().map_err(|e|e.refusal())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
            super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&engine,principal)?;
        } else { shared.dev_authority(&engine)?; }
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let floor = shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire);
        let effective_before = clock.now().max(floor);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = (if milliseconds == 0 { next.now() } else { next.advance(milliseconds)? }).max(floor);
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — refuse a saved boundary before any clock/session effect.
        if drain && !allow_held && !shared.hold_schedules {
            crate::dev_clock_store::refuse_held(&engine, &before, now)?;
        }
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        if !shared.personas {
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — a retained disclosure floor is also the credential authority clock.
            // A raw jump below that floor advances no business time and cannot
            // extend credentials or leases. Selection must never see an older
            // raw process time and revive an already expired family.
            let effective_delta = now.checked_sub(effective_before)
                .ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("effective clock advance overflow"))?;
            if milliseconds > 0 && effective_delta > 0 { engine.advance_staging_clock_at(effective_delta, effective_before, preserve_sessions).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?; }
        } else if milliseconds > 0 {
            engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?;
        }
        lock(&shared.maintenance).paused = true;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = clock.offset();
        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        if !drain { return Ok(Work::default()); }
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work_at(shared, true, 0, Some(now))?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            lock(&shared.maintenance).paused = !drain;
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":!drain, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced on failure. Stay paused until an explicit drain;
        // never retry an ambiguous advance to resume pending work.
        Err(error) => {
            let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
            let after = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
            let fired: Vec<_> = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().filter(|(i, _)| {
                matches!((before.get(*i).copied().flatten(), after.get(*i).copied().flatten()), (Some(old), Some(new)) if new > old)
            }).map(|(_, schedule)| schedule.name.clone()).collect();
            let mut schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, now, shared.hold_schedules || !drain, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            for (index, schedule) in schedules.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if schedule["fired"] != true && !shared.hold_schedules && before.get(index).copied().flatten().is_some_and(|old| old < engine.schema().schedules[index].boundary(now)) {
                    schedule["holdCode"] = "maintenance-failed".into();
                    schedule["reason"] = "clock advanced but maintenance stopped before this due boundary was queued; inspect clock and queued jobs before continuing; do not replay the advance".into();
                }
            }
            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
            respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":if preserve_sessions { crate::dev_clock::PRESERVED_SESSION_NOTICE } else { crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE }, "maintenancePaused":true, "sessionsPreserved":preserve_sessions, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held, "reportIncomplete":true, "denied":refusal_json(&error)}))
        },
    }
}

/// `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` with `{"args":{..}}`: queue one run of a job as the
/// system on the dev clock and drive chunks until it is done or parked. This is
/// what `snapback4 run <job>` forwards to while dev owns the store.
pub(super) fn run_job(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
    use snapback4_core::{JobState, error::Refusal, ir::ProgramKind, wire};
    let service_route=request.url().starts_with("/jobs/");
    let service=if service_route {
//! Bounded, read-only evidence from the store's own commit clock.
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

const SKIPPED_PREVIEW: usize = 32;
const TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 5_000;
const RECENT_COMMITS: usize = 64;
const TABLE_BYTES: usize = 4096;
const TABLE_NAMES: usize = 8;
const REPORT_ERROR_BYTES: usize = 1024;
// Reserve one MiB for the outer clock/work envelope inside the host encoder.
// The reply retains `schedules` and its fired/held partition.
const REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET: usize = (crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING - (1 << 20)) / 2;

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — inspect a fixed recent commit window, never application row values.
pub fn ahead(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Result<Option<String>, RuntimeError> {
    // Reverse primary-key traversal: no full scan, sort, row payloads or retained
    // historical stamp set. octet_length(column) uses SQLite record metadata;
    // unlike a text length/cast/substr, it need not load oversized payloads.
    let mut lead = 0;
    let mut tables = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut omitted = false;
    engine.store().visit_recent_commit_clock(now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS), TABLE_BYTES as i64, RECENT_COMMITS as i64, |record| {
        let at = record.at;
        if at <= now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS) { return Ok(()); }
        lead = lead.max(at.saturating_sub(now));
        let length = record.length.ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("commit clock record is missing touched length".into()))?;
        if length > TABLE_BYTES as i64 { omitted = true; return Ok(()); }
        let bytes = record.touched.ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("commit clock record is missing touched bytes".into()))?;
        match serde_json::from_slice::<Vec<String>>(&bytes) {
            Ok(names) => for name in names {
                if tables.contains(&name) { continue; }
                if tables.len() < TABLE_NAMES && name.len() <= 128 { tables.insert(name); }
                else { omitted = true; }
            },
            Err(_) => omitted = true,
        }
        Ok(())
    })?;
    if lead == 0 { return Ok(None); }
    let mut names = tables.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if omitted { names.push("additional tables omitted".into()); }
    if names.is_empty() { names.push("no table names recorded".into()); }
    Ok(Some(format!(
        "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD: recent commit stamps lead the current clock by up to {lead}ms (latest {RECENT_COMMITS} commits; tables: {}); use a fresh store per offset test ({}), or restart with the same --clock-offset (or dev --resume-clock) to continue",
        names.join(", "), crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())))
}

/// UTC calendar text for Unix milliseconds: schedule boundaries are UTC.
pub fn utc(ms: i64) -> String {
    let (days, rem) = (ms.div_euclid(86_400_000), ms.rem_euclid(86_400_000));
    // Civil-from-days (proleptic Gregorian), exact for the I53 range.
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z - era * 146_097;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1_460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let day = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let month = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let year = yoe + era * 400 + i64::from(month <= 2);
    format!("{year:04}-{month:02}-{day:02}T{:02}:{:02}:{:02}Z", rem / 3_600_000, rem % 3_600_000 / 60_000, rem % 60_000 / 1_000)
}

/// A positive duration in the units `clock advance` accepts, rounded up to a second.
pub fn span(ms: i64) -> String {
    let seconds = (ms.max(0) + 999) / 1_000;
    let (d, h, m, s) = (seconds / 86_400, seconds % 86_400 / 3_600, seconds % 3_600 / 60, seconds % 60);
    let parts: Vec<String> = [(d, "d"), (h, "h"), (m, "m"), (s, "s")].into_iter().filter(|(n, _)| *n > 0).map(|(n, unit)| format!("{n}{unit}")).collect();
    if parts.is_empty() { "0s".into() } else { parts.join("") }
}

/// Each schedule's saved boundary, in declaration order.
pub fn saved_boundaries(engine: &Engine) -> Vec<Option<i64>> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter()
        .map(|schedule| {
            let shape = engine.store().schedule_shape(&schedule.name).ok().flatten();
            if shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|shape| *shape != snapback4_core::value::digest(schedule)) { return None; }
            engine.store().schedule_boundary(&schedule.name).ok().flatten()
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Preflight is bounded before the clock or worker leases change.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — only explicit execution enumerates all crossed windows; coalesced reports keep a bounded preview.
pub fn crossed_boundaries(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool) -> Result<Vec<(usize, i64)>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    let mut boundaries = Vec::new();
    let mut report_bytes = 0usize;
    for (index, schedule) in engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate() {
        // JSON may escape every error byte; count that bound per schedule.
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add(2048 + 6 * (REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3) + 8 * (schedule.name.len() + schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule report exceeds its byte budget; reduce the declared schedules")); }
        if held { continue; }
        let Some(old) = before.get(index).copied().flatten() else { continue; };
        let end = schedule.boundary(now);
        if old >= end { continue; }
        let periods = schedule.periods_between(old, end);
        if each_boundary && periods > (10_000 - boundaries.len()) as i64 {
            return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("--each-boundary crosses more than 10000 schedule windows; use a smaller duration or omit --each-boundary to coalesce"));
        }
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add((if each_boundary { periods as usize } else { (periods as usize).min(SKIPPED_PREVIEW + 1) }).saturating_mul(1024 + 8 * schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule window report exceeds its byte budget; use a smaller duration")); }
        if !each_boundary { continue; }
        let mut boundary = schedule.next(old);
        while boundary <= end {
            boundaries.push((index, boundary));
            boundary = schedule.next(boundary);
        }
    }
    boundaries.sort_by_key(|(index, at)| (*at, *index));
    Ok(boundaries)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — no silent successful advance behind retained schedule markers.
pub fn refuse_held(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], now: i64) -> Result<(), snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    for (schedule, saved) in engine.schema().schedules.iter().zip(before) {
        if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| *saved >= schedule.boundary(now) && *saved > schedule.boundary(snapback4_core::now_ms())) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_HELD", "clock", format!(
                "schedule {} already processed boundary {}; requested clock {} has not reached its next window {}. Nothing advanced. Advance {} or use a fresh staging deployment; allowHeld:true explicitly accepts this hold. Never rewind saved boundaries",
                schedule.name, utc(saved), utc(now), utc(due), format!("{}ms", due - now))).guide("clock"));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn period(schedule: &snapback4_core::schema::Schedule) -> String {
    schedule.describe()
}

/// What each schedule did on one clock advance, and why one did not fire.
/// The core fires each schedule at most once per advance, for the latest
/// boundary, and holds it while its saved boundary is ahead of the clock.
// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — missed boundaries fire once; this only explains them.
pub fn schedule_report(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], fired: &[String], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool, port: Option<u16>) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
    let after = saved_boundaries(engine);
    // Read only bounded summaries: job args/cursors and full error payloads
    // are irrelevant to this report and must not be loaded then replicated.
    let runs = job_summaries(engine).unwrap_or_default();
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| {
        let (old, saved) = (before.get(index).copied().flatten(), after.get(index).copied().flatten());
        let current = schedule.boundary(now);
        let next = schedule.next(current);
        let mut entry = serde_json::json!({ "name": schedule.name, "target": schedule.target, "period": period(schedule), "fired": false, "firedWindows": [], "skippedBoundaries": [], "nextBoundary": utc(next) });
        if fired.contains(&schedule.name) {
            let end = saved.unwrap_or(current);
            entry["fired"] = true.into();
            let start = schedule.previous(end);
            let window = |boundary| serde_json::json!({"periodStart": schedule.previous(boundary), "periodEnd": boundary});
            let mut windows = Vec::new();
            if each_boundary {
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    let mut at = schedule.next(old);
                    while at <= end { windows.push(window(at)); at = schedule.next(at); }
                }
            } else { windows.push(window(end)); }
            if each_boundary {
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired {} windows: {}", windows.len(), windows.iter().map(|window| format!("{} to {}", utc(window["periodStart"].as_i64().unwrap()), utc(window["periodEnd"].as_i64().unwrap()))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")).into();
            }
            entry["firedWindows"] = windows.into();
            entry["periodStart"] = start.into();
            entry["periodEnd"] = end.into();
            if !each_boundary { entry["reason"] = format!("fired for the period {} to {}", utc(start), utc(end)).into(); }
            if let Some(run) = runs.get(&schedule.target) { entry["run"] = run.clone(); }
            // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — calendar periods count boundaries, not milliseconds.
            let skipped = if each_boundary { 0 } else { old.map(|old| schedule.periods_between(old, end) - 1).unwrap_or(0) };
            if skipped > 0 {
                entry["skippedPeriods"] = skipped.into();
                let mut windows = Vec::new();
                let mut at = schedule.next(old.unwrap());
                while at < end && windows.len() < SKIPPED_PREVIEW {
                    let mut item = window(at);
                    let mut args = serde_json::Map::new();
                    if let Some(program) = engine.program(&schedule.target) {
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") { args.insert("periodStart".into(), schedule.previous(at).into()); }
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") { args.insert("periodEnd".into(), at.into()); }
                    }
                    item["command"] = format!("snapback4 run {} '{}'{}", schedule.target, serde_json::Value::Object(args), port.map(|port| format!(" --port {port}")).unwrap_or_default()).into();
                    windows.push(item); at = schedule.next(at);
                }
                entry["skippedBoundariesOmitted"] = (skipped - windows.len() as i64).into();
                entry["skippedBoundaries"] = windows.into();
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired once, for the latest period {} to {}; {skipped} earlier period{} this advance crossed (from {}) did not run: missed boundaries fire once. Run {} with periodStart/periodEnd for an earlier period",
                    utc(start), utc(end), if skipped == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, utc(old.unwrap_or(end)), schedule.target).into();
            }
        } else if held {
            entry["holdCode"] = "disabled".into();
            entry["reason"] = "schedules are held (dev --hold-schedules or --replay); a restart without the flag resumes at the next boundary".into();
        } else if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| old.is_some() && (*saved > current || *saved == current && *saved > schedule.boundary(snapback4_core::now_ms()))) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            entry["holdCode"] = "saved-boundary".into();
            entry["savedBoundary"] = saved.into();
            entry["heldUntil"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["nextBoundary"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("held by the store: boundary {} was already processed and this clock {} has not reached its next window (restarting resets the process offset, not the saved boundary). One fire per boundary. Advance {} more, or use a fresh staging deployment/store for an independent test; keep the same --clock-offset when continuing a test across serve restarts",
                utc(saved), utc(now), span(due - now)).into();
        } else if old != saved {
            entry["holdCode"] = "initialized".into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("{} started at boundary {}: a schedule fires at the boundary after the first one it sees, next {} ({} from now)",
                if old.is_none() { "this schedule" } else { "this changed schedule" }, utc(saved.unwrap_or(current)), utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        } else {
            entry["holdCode"] = "not-due".into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("next boundary {} ({} from now; schedule times are UTC)", utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        }
        entry
    }).collect()
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — every declared schedule has one reported outcome.
pub fn outcomes(schedules: &[serde_json::Value]) -> (Vec<serde_json::Value>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {
    schedules.iter().cloned().partition(|schedule| schedule["fired"] == true)
}

/// The report never loads full error/argument payloads from retained jobs.
fn job_summaries(engine: &Engine) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut summaries = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    for summary in engine.store().job_summaries(REPORT_ERROR_BYTES)? {
        let name = summary.name;
        if summaries.contains_key(&name) { continue; }
        let mut error = summary.error;
        let truncated = summary.error_truncated;
        if let Some(text) = &mut error && truncated {
            let mut end = REPORT_ERROR_BYTES.min(text.len());
            while !text.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
            text.truncate(end); text.push('…');
        }
        summaries.insert(name, serde_json::json!({ "id":summary.id, "state":summary.state,
            "rowsWritten":summary.rows_written, "error":error, "errorTruncated":truncated }));
    }
    Ok(summaries)
}

/// Startup lines for schedules the store holds: their saved boundary leads the clock.
pub fn held_schedules(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Vec<String> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().zip(saved_boundaries(engine)).filter_map(|(schedule, saved)| {
        let saved = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > schedule.boundary(now))?;
        let due = schedule.next(saved);
        Some(format!("clock     schedule {} is held until {} ({} from now): its saved boundary {} is ahead of this clock; `clock advance {}` or restart with dev --resume-clock",
            schedule.name, utc(due), span(due - now), utc(saved), format!("{}ms", due - now)))
    }).collect()
}

/// The dev clock offset that continues a store's own time: its newest
/// recent commit stamp (or saved schedule boundary) less wall time, never negative.
pub fn resume_offset(store: &snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore, wall: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    let commit = store.recent_commit_max_at(RECENT_COMMITS as i64)?;
    let boundary = store.latest_schedule_boundary()?;
    let latest = commit.into_iter().chain(boundary).max().unwrap_or(wall);
    // Whole seconds, rounded up, so restarting twice does not drift backwards.
    Ok((latest.saturating_sub(wall).max(0) + 999) / 1_000 * 1_000)
}

pub fn has_data(engine: &Engine) -> bool {
    engine.store().inspection_has_data().unwrap_or(false)
}

pub fn offset_line(offset: i64, running: bool) -> String {
    format!("clock     offset from wall time: {offset:+}ms{}", if running { " (lasts until restart)" } else { " (no running dev; inspecting against wall time)" })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn engine() -> Engine {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table alpha:\n  at: time\n  public\ntable beta:\n  at: time\n  public\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program), 100_000).unwrap()
    }
    fn seed(engine: &mut Engine, table: &str, commit_at: i64, row_at: i64) {
        engine.seed_at([(table.into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
            ("id", snapback4_core::Value::Id(snapback4_core::ids::mint(row_at, &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).unwrap())),
            ("at", snapback4_core::Value::Int(row_at)),
        ]))], commit_at).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_bounds_one_large_job_error_repeated_across_many_schedules() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..500 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        assert_eq!(crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap().len(), 500);
        let fired = engine.fire_schedules(60_000).unwrap();
        let message = "🧪\n\\".repeat(30_000);
        (match engine.store() { snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::Sqlite(inner) => inner }).test_support_conn().execute("UPDATE _jobs SET error = ?1 WHERE id = (SELECT id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1)", [&message]).unwrap();
        let report = schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, 60_000, false, false, None);
        assert_eq!(report.len(), 500);
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["errorTruncated"] == true));
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["error"].as_str().unwrap().len() <= REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3));
        assert!(serde_json::to_vec(&report).unwrap().len() < 2 << 20);
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_refuses_before_the_host_encoder_limit() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..3000 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let refusal = crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_CLOCK_INPUT");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("byte budget"));
        assert_eq!(before, saved_boundaries(&engine));
        assert!(REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET < crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING);
    }
    #[test]
    fn utc_text_and_spans_match_the_calendar() {
        assert_eq!(utc(0), "1970-01-01T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(951_782_400_000), "2000-02-29T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(-1), "1969-12-31T23:59:59Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(1_791_468_000_000), "2026-10-08T14:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(span(12_633_000), "3h30m33s");
        assert_eq!(span(86_400_001), "1d1s");
        assert_eq!(span(0), "0s");
    }
    #[test]
    fn resume_offset_follows_the_newest_commit_and_is_never_negative() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 0);
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 3_700_500, 1);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 3_601_000);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 9_000_000).unwrap(), 0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn commit_tolerance_and_largest_lead_ignore_future_application_values() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 100_000, 1_000_000);
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 105_000, 2_000_000);
        seed(&mut engine, "beta", 107_000, 3_000_000);
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7000ms (latest 64 commits; tables: beta)"), "{note}");
        let note = ahead(&engine, 99_999).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7001ms (latest 64 commits; tables: alpha, beta)"), "{note}");
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 102_000).unwrap().is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn recent_window_does_not_claim_older_commits_or_read_row_payloads() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 200_000, 200_000);
        for n in 0..RECENT_COMMITS { seed(&mut engine, "beta", 100_000, 300_000 + n as i64); }
        // Deliberately unreadable images: metadata inspection must not deserialize them.
        (match engine.store() { snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::Sqlite(inner) => inner }).test_support_conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data = 'not json'", []).unwrap();
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        (match engine.store() { snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::Sqlite(inner) => inner }).test_support_conn().execute("UPDATE _commits SET at = 200000, touched = ?1 WHERE seq = (SELECT MAX(seq) FROM _commits)", ["x".repeat(TABLE_BYTES + 1)]).unwrap();
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("100000ms") && note.contains("additional tables omitted"), "{note}");
    }
}
