//! Store-neutral authority decisions over typed transaction capabilities.
//!
//! Storage adapters expose identity and work facts. This module alone walks
//! admission families and decides whether a session or claim still stands.

use crate::RuntimeError;

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) mod test_cases;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Authority {
    pub family: String,
    pub generation: i64,
    pub expires_at: i64,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct SessionRecord {
    pub principal: String,
    pub family: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub digest: String,
    pub scope: SessionScope,
    pub cap_expires_at: Option<i64>,
    pub principal_generation: Option<i64>,
    pub expires_at: i64,
}

impl SessionRecord {
    pub(crate) fn identity_is_valid(&self) -> bool {
        refuse_nul(&[(&self.principal, "this session is not live; sign in again"),
            (&self.kind, "this session is not live; sign in again"),
            (&self.family, "this session is not live; sign in again")]).is_ok()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct WorkRecord {
    pub principal: String,
    pub state: String,
    pub claimed_by: Option<String>,
    pub session_digest: Option<String>,
    pub lease_until: Option<i64>,
    pub deadline: Option<i64>,
    pub admission: AdmissionRecord,
    pub service_generation: Option<i64>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct AdmissionRecord {
    pub family: Option<String>,
    pub generation: Option<i64>,
    pub expires_at: Option<i64>,
}

/// The durable facts the authority kernel may read in one store transaction.
/// Implementations perform access only; the policy below interprets records.
pub(crate) trait AuthorityTransaction {
    fn principal_generation(&self, principal: &str) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError>;
    fn session_by_digest(&self, digest: &str) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    fn latest_live_family_member(
        &self,
        principal: &str,
        family: &str,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    fn work_by_id(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<WorkRecord>, RuntimeError>;
    fn work_by_claimed_session_digest(
        &self,
        digest: &str,
    ) -> Result<Option<WorkRecord>, RuntimeError>;

    fn invalidate_session(&self, digest: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
    fn invalidate_family(&self, principal: &str, family: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
}

pub(crate) enum AdmissionStanding {
    SessionNotLive,
    AuthorityNotLive,
    Live(Authority),
}

/// Resolve the credential captured at admission. Refresh may replace its row,
/// but the family and principal generation remain the durable authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — action authority is bound at admission and walked inside one transaction.
pub(crate) fn admission_authority(
    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    token_digest: &str,
    principal: &str,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<AdmissionStanding, RuntimeError> {
    let Some(session) = tx.session_by_digest(token_digest)? else {
        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::SessionNotLive);
    };
    if !session.identity_is_valid() || session.scope != SessionScope::Full || session.principal != principal || session.expires_at <= now {
        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::SessionNotLive);
    }
    let generation = tx.principal_generation(principal)?;
    let Some(standing) = family_authority_expiry(tx, principal, &session.family, generation, now)?
    else {
        return Ok(AdmissionStanding::AuthorityNotLive);
    };
    Ok(AdmissionStanding::Live(Authority {
        family: session.family,
        generation,
        expires_at: session.expires_at.min(standing),
    }))
}

/// Walk a refresh/delegation family at one transaction snapshot. The admitted
/// expiry is deliberately not an ongoing cap: refresh lifetime and admission
/// lifetime are distinct. Claim leases and deadlines remain hard caps.
pub(crate) fn family_authority_expiry(
    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    principal: &str,
    family: &str,
    generation: i64,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
    if refuse_nul(&[(principal, "identity is not live"), (family, "identity is not live")]).is_err() { return Ok(None); }
    let current_generation = tx.principal_generation(principal)?;
    let (mut family, mut generation, mut bound) = (family.to_owned(), generation, i64::MAX);
    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    loop {
        if refuse_nul(&[(&family, "identity is not live")]).is_err()
            || generation != current_generation || !seen.insert(family.clone()) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let Some(session) = tx.latest_live_family_member(principal, &family, now)? else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        if !session.identity_is_valid() || session.scope != SessionScope::Full { return Ok(None); }
        bound = bound.min(session.expires_at);
        if session.kind != "action" {
            return Ok(Some(bound));
        }
        let Some(parent) = tx.work_by_claimed_session_digest(&session.digest)? else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        if parent.principal != principal || !claim_window_is_live(&parent, now) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let lease = parent.lease_until.expect("a live claim has a lease");
        bound = bound.min(lease).min(parent.deadline.unwrap_or(i64::MAX));
        if let Some(service_generation) = parent.service_generation {
            return Ok((service_generation == current_generation).then_some(bound));
        }
        match (parent.admission.family, parent.admission.generation) {
            (Some(next), Some(next_generation)) => {
                family = next;
                generation = next_generation;
            }
            (None, None) => return Ok(Some(bound)), // Legacy work and development personas.
            _ => return Ok(None),
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn work_authority_expiry(
    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    work: &WorkRecord,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
    if refuse_nul(&[(&work.principal, "identity is not live"),
        (work.admission.family.as_deref().unwrap_or(""), "identity is not live")]).is_err() { return Ok(None); }
    if let Some(generation) = work.service_generation {
        return Ok((tx.principal_generation(&work.principal)? == generation).then_some(i64::MAX));
    }
    match (&work.admission.family, work.admission.generation, work.admission.expires_at) {
        (Some(family), Some(generation), Some(_)) => family_authority_expiry(
            tx,
            &work.principal,
            family,
            generation,
            now,
        ),
        _ => Ok(Some(i64::MAX)), // Legacy rows and development personas.
    }
}

fn claim_window_is_live(work: &WorkRecord, now: i64) -> bool {
    work.state == "running"
        && work.lease_until.is_some_and(|lease| lease > now)
        && work.deadline.is_none_or(|deadline| deadline > now)
}

pub(crate) fn claim_is_owned(work: &WorkRecord, worker: &str, digest: &str) -> bool {
    work.claimed_by.as_deref() == Some(worker) && work.session_digest.as_deref() == Some(digest)
}

pub(crate) fn live_claim_authority(
    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    digest: &str,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    let Some(work) = tx.work_by_claimed_session_digest(digest)? else {
        return Ok(false);
    };
    Ok(claim_window_is_live(&work, now) && work_authority_expiry(tx, &work, now)?.is_some())
}

pub(crate) fn held_claim_authority(
    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    id: &str,
    worker: &str,
    digest: &str,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    let Some(work) = tx.work_by_id(id)? else {
        return Ok(false);
    };
    if !claim_is_owned(&work, worker, digest) {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    live_claim_authority(tx, digest, now)
}

pub(crate) fn live_session(
    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    digest: &str,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<Option<SessionRecord>, RuntimeError> {
    let Some(session) = tx.session_by_digest(digest)? else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    if !session.identity_is_valid() || session.expires_at <= now {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — revocation generations survive an older executable.
    if session.scope == SessionScope::Read
        && (session.cap_expires_at.is_none_or(|cap| cap <= now)
            || session.principal_generation.is_none()
            || session.principal_generation.unwrap() < tx.principal_generation(&session.principal)?) {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if session.kind == "action" && !live_claim_authority(tx, digest, now)? {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    Ok(Some(session))
}

pub(crate) fn live_service_session(
    tx: &impl AuthorityTransaction,
    digest: &str,
    principal: &str,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(tx.session_by_digest(digest)?.is_some_and(|session| {
        session.identity_is_valid() && session.scope == SessionScope::Full && session.principal == principal && session.kind == "service" && session.expires_at > now
    }))
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum SessionScope { Full, Read }

// @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#9.1 — identity fails closed on both stores, including pre-upgrade SQLite rows.
// Lives here, not in `identity`, because authority is compiled without the SQLite feature.
pub(crate) fn refuse_nul(values: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    for (value, message) in values {
        if value.contains('\0') { return Err(RuntimeError::Auth((*message).into())); }
    }
    Ok(())
}
        };
        lock(&shared.maintenance).completed(Instant::now(), started.consumed(), budget == CONTENDED_TIME || shared.engine.has_waiters(), result.as_ref().map_or(true, |worked| *worked));
        phase(shared, result, false, "bulk maintenance").unwrap_or(false)
    })
}

fn timely_engine_work<T: Default>(shared: &Shared, run: impl FnOnce(&mut snapback4_core::Engine, Duration) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
    let result = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| {
        if { let state = lock(&shared.maintenance); state.advancing || state.paused } { return Ok(T::default()); }
        run(engine, budget)
    });
    promote_aged(shared, Instant::now());
    result
}

fn timely(shared: &Shared, tick: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.sweep_expired_for(shared.now(), 2_000, budget)), false, "the expiry sweep")?;
    if !shared.hold_schedules {
        let count = timely_engine_work(shared, |engine, _| Ok(engine.schema().schedules.len()))?;
        for index in 0..count {
            // One atomic boundary per admission, never the entire schedule set.
            // Adoption between slices is harmless: the next tick visits its set.
            let fired = phase(shared, timely_engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_at(shared.now(), index)), false, "schedules")?;
            for name in fired { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: schedule {name} fired"); }
        }
    }

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — presentation preserves precise read evidence.
export function ReadState<R extends readonly unknown[]>({ read: source, loading = null, empty = null, denied, children }: {
  read: Read<R> | { read: Read<R> };
  loading?: ReactNode;
  empty?: ReactNode;
  denied?: (why: Refusal) => ReactNode;
  children: (items: R, read: Ready<R>) => ReactNode;
}): ReactNode {
  const read = "read" in source ? source.read : source;
  if ("denied" in read) {
    if (denied) return denied(read.denied);
    throw new QueryRefusal(read.denied);
  }
  if (!("data" in read)) return loading;
  return read.data.length === 0 ? empty : children(read.data, read);
}

/** Authorized data as it arrives, undefined while loading. Null and empty
 * arrays remain data. Partial/stale answers remain data too: use useQuery or
 * usePage when completeness, freshness or continuation matters. Refusals throw
 * QueryRefusal; useQueryResult instead for an inline typed refusal. */
export function useQueryData<A, R>(op: Query<A, R>, args: A, options?: ReadHookOptions): R | undefined {
  const read = useQuery(op, args, options);
  if ("denied" in read) throw new QueryRefusal(read.denied);
  return "data" in read ? read.data : undefined;
}

// in auth::parse_bearer does (HT, LF, FF, CR, space; not JS trim's Unicode set).
// This is a prefix check, not authority. Keep the supplied credential unchanged.
export const isReadToken = (token?: string): boolean => /^[\t\n\f\r ]*ro\./.test(token ?? "");

pub(crate) fn preserves_only_live(tx: &(impl SessionClockTransaction + OutboxTransaction)) {
    preserve(tx, 1_000, 100).unwrap();
    tx.shift_worker_leases(1_000, 100).unwrap();
    let full = tx.session_by_digest("full").unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(full.expires_at,1_200);
    let read = tx.session_by_digest("read").unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!((read.expires_at,read.cap_expires_at),(1_200,Some(1_220)));
    for digest in ["expired","cap-expired","revoked-read","closed-action"] {
        assert!(authority::live_session(tx,digest,1_100).unwrap().is_none(),"{digest} must stay dead");
    }
    assert_eq!(tx.session_by_digest("expired").unwrap().unwrap().expires_at,100);
    assert_eq!(tx.session_by_digest("cap-expired").unwrap().unwrap().cap_expires_at,Some(100));
    assert_eq!(tx.session_by_digest("revoked-read").unwrap().unwrap().expires_at,200);
    assert_eq!(tx.session_by_digest("worker").unwrap().unwrap().expires_at,1_200,"supervised family shifts exactly once");
    let claim = tx.work_by_id("running").unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(claim.lease_until,Some(1_180));
    assert_eq!(claim.deadline,Some(150),"product deadlines advance normally");
    assert!(authority::live_session(tx,"action",1_100).unwrap().is_none(),"the unchanged product deadline still fences the claim");
    let mints = tx.clock_mints(None).unwrap();
    for mint in mints {
        assert_eq!(mint.expires_at,if mint.intent == "live" { 1_200 } else if mint.intent == "expired" { 100 } else { 200 },"mint {}",mint.intent);
    }
    let row = tx.clock_sessions(false,None).unwrap().into_iter().find(|r| r.digest == "full").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(row.created_at,1_010,"refresh duration keeps its original width");
}

pub(crate) fn overflow_is_validated_before_writes(tx: &impl SessionClockTransaction) {
    let err = preserve(tx,i64::MAX,100).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(err.code(),"E_INPUT");
    assert_eq!(tx.session_by_digest("full").unwrap().unwrap().expires_at,200);
    assert_eq!(tx.session_by_digest("read").unwrap().unwrap().cap_expires_at,Some(220));
    assert_eq!(tx.clock_mints(None).unwrap().into_iter().find(|m| m.intent == "live").unwrap().expires_at,200);
}

import { pendingRead, readyRead } from "./read-existence.ts";
import type { Client, Query, Read, Observed, ObserveOptions, Cursor } from "./contract.ts";
import { isReady } from "./contract.ts";
import { peekObservation } from "./observe-watch.ts";
const LOADING: Read<never> = { loading: true };

/** The requested page chain, with one newest observed image per row ID. */
export class Pages<A extends { c?: Cursor | null }, R extends readonly unknown[]> {
  private pages: { cursor: Cursor | null; observed: Observed<R>; unsubscribe: () => void }[] = [];
  private requested = 1;
  private subs = new Set<() => void>();
  private cache: { items: R[number][]; hasMore: boolean; read: Read<R> } | null = null;
  private peeked: Read<R> | undefined;
  private cacheReads: Read<R>[] = [];
  private reconciling = false;
  private imageRevision = 0;
  private imageRevisions = new WeakMap<object, number>();
  private noteImage(read: Read<R>) {
    // Revalidation/freshness changes retain the same data array; they do not
    // make an older row image newer than an image delivered on another page.
    if (isReady(read) && Array.isArray(read.data) && !this.imageRevisions.has(read.data))
      this.imageRevisions.set(read.data, ++this.imageRevision);
    return read;
  }
  constructor(private client: Client, private op: Query<A, R>, private args: A, private options?: ObserveOptions) {}
  private open(c: Cursor | null) {
    const observed = this.client.observe(this.op, { ...this.args, c }, this.options);
    const page = { cursor: c, observed, unsubscribe: () => {} };
    this.pages.push(page);
    page.unsubscribe = observed.subscribe(() => {
      if (this.pages.includes(page)) this.notify();
    });
  }
  private drop(from: number) {
    for (const page of this.pages.splice(from)) {
      page.unsubscribe();
      page.observed.close();
    }
  }
  private notify() {
    if (this.reconciling) return;
    this.reconciling = true;
    try {
      // A later page belongs to its predecessor's current cursor. Keep the
      // requested depth, but discard the old suffix before opening a new one:
      // deduplication alone cannot recover rows an old boundary skipped.
      for (let i = 0; i < this.pages.length; i++) {
        const read = this.noteImage(this.pages[i]!.observed.getSnapshot());
        const next = isReady(read) && Array.isArray(read.data) ? read.next : null;
        if (!next || i + 1 >= this.requested || this.pages.slice(0, i + 1).some((p) => p.cursor === next)) {
          this.drop(i + 1);
          break;
        }
        if (this.pages[i + 1]?.cursor !== next) {
          this.drop(i + 1);
          this.open(next);
        }
      }
      this.cache = null;
    } finally { this.reconciling = false; }
    for (const s of this.subs) s();
  }
  subscribe = (notify: () => void) => {
    this.subs.add(notify);
    if (!this.pages.length) { this.open(null); this.notify(); }
    let active = true;
    return () => {
      if (!active) return;
      active = false;
      this.subs.delete(notify);
      if (!this.subs.size) { this.drop(0); this.cache = null; }
    };
  };
  getSnapshot = () => {
    const peeked = this.pages.length ? undefined : peekObservation(this.client, this.op, { ...this.args, c: null }, this.options);
    // An unsubscribed render owns no notifications: recheck the live snapshot
    // and the linger cutoff on every call, caching only its derived page shape.
    if (peeked !== this.peeked) { this.peeked = peeked; this.cache = null; }
    // @ref LLP 3000.023#5 — page accessors check deadlines before a combined cache can escape.
    let sources, reads: Read<R>[];
    do {
      sources = [...this.pages];
      reads = sources.length ? sources.map(page => this.noteImage(page.observed.getSnapshot())) : peeked ? [this.noteImage(peeked)] : [];
      // A deadline check may synchronously drop a suffix through notify().
    } while (sources.length !== this.pages.length || sources.some((page, i) => page !== this.pages[i]));
    if (reads.length !== this.cacheReads.length || reads.some((read, i) => read !== this.cacheReads[i])) this.cache = null;
    this.cacheReads = reads;
    if (this.cache) return this.cache;
    const items: R[number][] = [];
    const identities = new Map<string, { index: number; revision: number }>();
    let last: Read<R> = LOADING as Read<R>;
    let complete = true, fresh = true, revalidating = false, pending = false;
    let cut: import("./contract.ts").ReadCut = null;
    let since: number | undefined;
    let knownSince = true;
    let loadingMore = false;
    for (const read of reads) {
      // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.3 — a pending next page keeps the ready prefix on screen.
      // Resets of the first page and refusals still replace the combined read.
      if ("loading" in read && isReady(last)) { loadingMore = true; break; }
      last = read;
      if (!isReady(read)) break;
      if (!Array.isArray(read.data)) { last = LOADING as Read<R>; break; }
      // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.3 — keyset edits may repeat IDs across pages.
      // Keep the first display position and the most recently observed image,
      // including a refreshed earlier page. Only compiler-proved source row IDs are accumulation keys; arbitrary
      // projections preserve ordinary list semantics, including a field named id.
      const revision = this.imageRevisions.get(read.data)!;
      for (const item of read.data as readonly R[number][]) {
        const id = this.op.rowKey === "id" && item && typeof item === "object" && "id" in item ? item.id : undefined;
        const previous = typeof id === "string" ? identities.get(id) : undefined;
        if (previous) {
          if (revision >= previous.revision) {
            items[previous.index] = item;
            previous.revision = revision;
          }
        } else {
          if (typeof id === "string") identities.set(id, { index: items.length, revision });
          items.push(item);
        }
      }
      pending ||= pendingRead(read);
      complete &&= read.complete;
      if (read.cut === "meter" || (cut === null && read.cut === "words")) cut = read.cut;
      fresh &&= read.fresh;
      revalidating ||= read.revalidating === true;
      if (!read.fresh) {
        if (read.since == null) knownSince = false;
        else since = Math.min(since ?? read.since, read.since);
      }
    }
    const hasMore = !loadingMore && isReady(last) && last.next != null && !this.pages.some((p) => p.cursor === last.next);
    const read: Read<R> = isReady(last)
      ? readyRead({ data: items as unknown as R, complete, cut, fresh, ...(revalidating ? { revalidating: true } : {}), ...(knownSince && since != null ? { since } : {}), next: last.next ?? null }, pending || loadingMore)
      : last;
    this.cache = { items, hasMore, read };
    return this.cache;
  };
  loadMore = () => {
    if (!this.subs.size || !this.getSnapshot().hasMore) return;
    this.requested = Math.max(this.requested, this.pages.length + 1);
    this.notify();
  };
}
// The link watchdog belongs to the transport, shared by both client tiers.
// A stalled fetch must not depend on the host eventually rejecting it.
import { sampleChanges } from "./clock.ts";
import type { EphemeralVersion } from "./ephemeral-reads.ts";
import type { Refusal } from "./contract.ts";
import { retryAfter } from "./http-response.ts";

const SILENCE_MS = 4000;
const HEARTBEAT_SECONDS = 2;
// @ref LLP 3000.036#6-idle-live-clients-must-not-cost-a-thread — a full long-wait bound degrades to unheld polls, never to offline.
/** After E_STREAMS, poll without holding for this long, at the server's Retry-After pace. */
const UNHELD_MS = 30_000;
interface Change { changes?: unknown; preserved?: unknown; ephemeral?: EphemeralVersion & { tables: string[] }; serverNow?: number; clockRevision?: string; seq: number; tables: string[]; generation?: number; store_id?: string; denied?: Refusal }

export function changeFeed(fetchNow: typeof fetch, base: string, lifetime: AbortSignal, offline: () => void, online: () => void) {
  const active = new Set<AbortController>();
  const abort = () => { for (const request of active) request.abort(); };
  const lost = () => { offline(); abort(); };
  const events = typeof globalThis.addEventListener === "function";
  if (events) {
    globalThis.addEventListener("offline", lost);
    globalThis.addEventListener("online", online);
  }
  const close = () => {
    abort();
    if (events) {
      globalThis.removeEventListener("offline", lost);
      globalThis.removeEventListener("online", online);
    }
  };
  lifetime.addEventListener("abort", close, { once: true });
  // E_STREAMS means the host (503) or this principal's share (429) has no
  // room for another held poll. The poll loop already waits Retry-After for
  // the retryable refusal; until UNHELD_MS passes, this feed then asks
  // without `wait` at that pace, so live queries keep learning of commits.
  let unheldUntil = 0, unheldEvery = 0, paceNext = false;
  // The pace is a delay before the request, inside the same fetch promise:
  // the call's own abort ends it, and its silence watchdog starts after it.
  let paced: { ms: number; started: () => void } | undefined;
  const doFetch = (input: string, init: RequestInit): Promise<Response> => {
    const pace = paced;
    paced = undefined;
    if (!pace) return fetchNow(input, init);
    return new Promise<void>(resolve => {
      const signal = init.signal;
      if (signal?.aborted) { resolve(); return; }
      const done = () => { clearTimeout(timer); signal?.removeEventListener("abort", done); resolve(); };
      const timer = setTimeout(done, pace.ms);
      signal?.addEventListener("abort", done, { once: true });
    }).then(() => { pace.started(); return fetchNow(input, init); });
  };
  return async (seq: number, wait: number, headers: Record<string, string>, storeIdentity?: string, ephemeral?: { epoch: number; incarnation: string; tables: string[] }, restart?: AbortSignal) => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    active.add(controller);
    const reissue = () => controller.abort();
    restart?.addEventListener("abort", reissue, { once: true });
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    let reader: ReadableStreamDefaultReader<Uint8Array> | undefined;
    const aborted = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
      controller.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => reject(controller.signal.reason), { once: true });
    });
    const arm = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(new Error("changes: no response bytes for 4000 ms")), SILENCE_MS);
    };
    // React Native fetch implementations without response streams buffer the
    // entire body. Use short polls there so a healthy idle link still speaks
    // within the same watchdog interval.
    const streaming = typeof ReadableStream !== "undefined" && typeof Response !== "undefined" && "body" in Response.prototype;
    const unheld = Date.now() < unheldUntil;
    const seconds = unheld ? 0 : streaming ? wait : Math.min(wait, HEARTBEAT_SECONDS);
    if (unheld && paceNext) paced = { ms: unheldEvery, started: arm };
    else arm();
    paceNext = false;
    try {
      if (lifetime.aborted || restart?.aborted) controller.abort();
      const parameters = ephemeral ? `&eph=${ephemeral.epoch}&ephi=${encodeURIComponent(ephemeral.incarnation)}&ephTables=${encodeURIComponent(ephemeral.tables.join(","))}` : "";
      const work = (async () => {
        const response = await doFetch(`${base}/changes?since=${seq}&wait=${seconds}${streaming ? "&heartbeat=1" : ""}${storeIdentity === undefined ? "" : `&store_id=${encodeURIComponent(storeIdentity)}`}${parameters}`, { headers, signal: controller.signal });
        if (controller.signal.aborted) throw controller.signal.reason;
        arm();
        let json: Change;
        if (response.body?.getReader) {
          reader = response.body.getReader();
          const decoder = new TextDecoder();
          let text = "";
          for (;;) {
            const { value, done } = await reader.read();
            if (controller.signal.aborted) throw controller.signal.reason;
            if (done) break;
            if (value.length) arm();
            text += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
            if (!text.trim()) text = ""; // discard whitespace heartbeat frames
            if (text.length > 16 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error("changes: response exceeds 16 MiB");
          }
          json = JSON.parse(text + decoder.decode()) as Change;
        } else json = await response.json() as Change;
        sampleChanges(response, json);
        if (json.denied?.code === "E_STREAMS") {
          unheldUntil = Date.now() + UNHELD_MS;
          unheldEvery = Math.max(1000, retryAfter(response.headers.get("retry-after")));
        } else if (unheld) paceNext = true;
        return { response, json };
      })();
      return await Promise.race([work, aborted]);
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
      void reader?.cancel().catch(() => {});
      active.delete(controller);
      restart?.removeEventListener("abort", reissue);
    }
  };
}
    if (readGate) {
      const why = await readGate.verify();
      if (why) return Response.json({ denied: why }, { status: 403 });
      const sent = new Headers(args[1]?.headers).get("authorization");
      if (sent !== headers.authorization) return Response.json({ denied: auth() }, { status: 401 });
    }
    const response = await transport(...args);
    release?.hint(response);
    const resetStore = response.headers.get("x-snapback-dev-reset");
    if (resetStore && storeIdentity !== undefined && resetStore !== storeIdentity) stopForStore("E_DEV_RESET");
    if (!closed && response.headers.get("x-snapback-ephemeral") === "1" && !ephemeralAdvertised) {
      ephemeralAdvertised = true;
      backendLoaded = false;
      ephemeralReads.reissuePoll();
    }
    if (response.headers.get("x-snapback-settlement") === "1") settlementAdvertised = true;
    return response;
  };
  const base = options.url.replace(/\/$/, "");
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { "content-type": "application/json" };
  if (options.persona) headers["x-snapback-persona"] = options.persona;
  let session = options.persona ? null : initialSession ?? null;
  const token = options.persona ? undefined : session?.token ?? supplied.token ?? options.token;
  if (token) headers.authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  let viewer: Principal | null = options.persona ? (`dev:${options.persona}` as Principal) : initialSession ? (initialSession.principal as Principal) : null;
  let credentials = Boolean(viewer || token);
  let link: Link = credentials ? "online" : "signed-out";
  let closed = false;
  let delivery: ReturnType<typeof onlineDelivery> | undefined;
  const aborter = new AbortController();
  const downloads = assetQueue();
  const requests = new Set<Promise<unknown>>();
  let closing: Promise<void> | undefined;
  const closingResponses = new ClosingResponses();
  let seq = 0;
  let minimumReadSeq = 0, queryEpoch = 0;
  const queryChanges = new QueryCuts();
  let queryBackend: BackendJson | undefined, settlementAdvertised = false, queryGeneration = 0;
  let storeIdentity: string | undefined;
  const readGate = options.readOnly ? new ReadScopeGate(base, transport,
    () => ({ token: headers.authorization?.slice(7), revision: credentialRevision, store: storeIdentity, closed: closed || !!storeRefusal || !credentials }),
    json => { observeStore(json, minimumReadSeq); if (typeof json.viewer === "string") setViewer(json.viewer as Principal); }, aborter.signal) : undefined;
  const dispatch: DispatchState = { identify: async () => {
    if (!storeIdentity && !probed) await waitForCaller(probe(), aborter.signal);
  } };
  let probed = false;
  let storeRefusal: Refusal | null = null;
  const linkListeners = new Set<(link: Link) => void>();
  const sessionListeners = new Set<(session: Session | null) => void>();
  const stopSessionTiming = clock.beforeSample((response, request) => {
    if (closed || !credentials || !session || !requestMatchesSession(request, base, session)) return;
    const next = reconciledTiming(session, response);
    if (next === session) return;
    session = next;
    for (const listener of [...sessionListeners]) listener(session);
  });
//! Timing-critical maintenance and bulk catch-up share bounded engine slices.
use super::{Shared, lock, refusal_json, respond};
use serde_json::json;
use snapback4_core::RuntimeError;
use std::{sync::Arc, time::{Duration, Instant}};
use tiny_http::Request;

#[derive(Default, serde::Serialize)]
struct Work { delivery_chunks: usize, expired: u64, schedules: usize, chunks: usize, parked: usize, #[serde(skip)] fired: Vec<String> }

const WORK_TIME: Duration = Duration::from_millis(2);
const CONTENDED_TIME: Duration = Duration::from_micros(250);

// Charge consumed CPU for pacing, not time the OS already kept this thread off
// CPU. The cooperative chunk deadline still uses wall time. A failed clock
// sample falls back to the more conservative wall-time pause.
struct SliceClock { wall: Instant, cpu: Option<Duration> }
impl SliceClock {
    fn start() -> Self { Self { wall: Instant::now(), cpu: Self::cpu_time() } }
    fn cpu_time() -> Option<Duration> {
        let mut at = libc::timespec { tv_sec: 0, tv_nsec: 0 };
        if unsafe { libc::clock_gettime(libc::CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID, &mut at) } != 0 { return None; }
        Some(Duration::new(at.tv_sec.try_into().ok()?, at.tv_nsec.try_into().ok()?))
    }
    fn consumed(&self) -> Duration {
        self.cpu.zip(Self::cpu_time()).and_then(|(start, end)| end.checked_sub(start)).unwrap_or_else(|| self.wall.elapsed())
    }
}

// Detached readers do not take engine tickets. Sample OS load as well as the
// round-3 engine contention signal; cache the syscall for 100 ms. Load includes
// other tenants and trails short bursts, so this is a pacing hint, not admission.
fn host_contended() -> bool {
    use std::sync::{Mutex, OnceLock};
    static SAMPLE: Mutex<(Option<Instant>, bool)> = Mutex::new((None, false));
    static CPUS: OnceLock<usize> = OnceLock::new();
    let mut sample = lock(&SAMPLE);
    if sample.0.is_none_or(|at| at.elapsed() >= Duration::from_millis(100)) {
        let mut load = 0.0;
        let cpus = *CPUS.get_or_init(|| std::thread::available_parallelism().map_or(1, |n| n.get()));
        sample.1 = unsafe { libc::getloadavg(&mut load, 1) } == 1 && load >= cpus as f64;
        sample.0 = Some(Instant::now());
    }
    sample.1
}

fn engine_work<T>(shared: &Shared, run: impl FnOnce(&mut snapback4_core::Engine, Duration) -> T) -> T {
    let mut engine = shared.engine.background();
    lock(&shared.readers).retry_missing(&engine);
    let budget = if engine.contended() || host_contended() { CONTENDED_TIME } else { WORK_TIME };
    let result = run(&mut engine, budget);
    // Publish from this guard: a second ordinary admission would both delay
    // notification and mistake maintenance for a newly arrived request.
    shared.publish_engine(&engine);
    result
}

fn phase<T: Default>(shared: &Shared, result: Result<T, RuntimeError>, drain: bool, name: &str) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
    match result {
        Err(error) if !drain => { super::adoption::background_failure(shared, name, &error); Ok(T::default()) }
        other => other,
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — lifetimes are swept into the partition log; missed schedule boundaries fire once.
fn work(shared: &Shared, drain: bool, tick: u64) -> Result<Work, RuntimeError> {
    work_at(shared, drain, tick, None)
}

fn work_at(shared: &Shared, drain: bool, tick: u64, schedule_sample: Option<i64>) -> Result<Work, RuntimeError> {
    let mut work = Work::default();
    shared.ephemeral.sweep(shared.now())?;
    work.delivery_chunks = delivery(shared, drain)?;
    loop {
        if phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.run_view_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "view recount")?.is_none() {break;}
        work.chunks += 1;
        if !drain && work.chunks >= 500 {break;}
        std::thread::yield_now();
    }
    loop {
        let swept = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.sweep_expired_for(shared.now(), 2_000, budget)), drain, "the expiry sweep")?;
        work.expired += swept;
        if !drain || swept == 0 { break; }
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — advancing a replay clock does not release its schedule hold.
    if !shared.hold_schedules {
        let fired = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, _| {
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.2 — select advance windows before draining; admission deadlines still use the live advanced clock.
            if let Some(sample) = schedule_sample {
                let windows = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| (index, schedule.boundary(sample))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                let mut fired = Vec::new();
                for (index, boundary) in windows { fired.extend(engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, boundary)?); }
                Ok(fired)
            } else { engine.fire_schedules(shared.now()) }
        }), drain, "schedules")?;
        work.schedules = fired.len();
        for name in &fired { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: schedule {name} fired"); }
        work.fired = fired;
    }
    while drain || work.chunks < 500 {
        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { break; }
        let (run, logs) = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| {
            let run = engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget);
            (run, engine.take_native_logs())
        });
        for line in logs { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
        let Some(run) = phase(shared, run, drain, "job")? else { break; };
        work.chunks += 1;
        match run.state {
            snapback4_core::JobState::Done => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} done: {} chunk{}, {} row{} written", run.name, run.chunks, if run.chunks == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, run.rows_written, if run.rows_written == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
            snapback4_core::JobState::Parked => {
                work.parked += 1;
                if shared.personas { shared.refusals.job(&run); }
                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} parked after {} chunks: {}", run.name, run.chunks, run.error.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        // FIFO engine admission hands waiting requests their turn before the
        // next chunk; yield_now alone cannot make that promise for a mutex.
    }
    if drain || tick % 5 == 0 {
        loop {
            let actions = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.requeue_expired_actions_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "the action lease sweep")?;
            let effects = phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.requeue_expired_effects_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "the effect lease sweep")?;
            if actions + effects > 0 { shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine)); }

  const readyNotifications = new Map<Watch, readonly (() => void)[]>();
  let readyNotificationTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  let readyNotificationAt = Infinity;
  function cancelReadyNotification() {
    if (readyNotificationTimer !== undefined) clearTimeout(readyNotificationTimer);
    readyNotificationTimer = undefined;
    readyNotificationAt = Infinity;
  }
  function flushReadyNotifications() {
    cancelReadyNotification();
    const ready = [...readyNotifications]; readyNotifications.clear();
    for (const [watch, subscribers] of ready) {
      if (watches.get(watch.key) !== watch || !("data" in watch.snapshot)) continue;
      for (const subscriber of subscribers) if (watch.subscribers.has(subscriber)) {
        try { subscriber(); } catch (error) { console.error("snapback4 client listener:", error); }
      }
    }
  }
  // @ref LLP 3000.023#10 — coalesce ready notifications under pressure, never authorization or withdrawals.
  function notifyReady(watch: Watch, previousDeadline: number | undefined) {
    readyNotifications.set(watch, [...watch.subscribers]);
    if (!readWork.hasPending) { flushReadyNotifications(); return; }
    // Publication retires the old episode timer. Its displayed answer still
    // needs a notification by that same bound, even when other work is held.
    const now = Date.now();
    const at = Math.min(now + (typeof document === "undefined" ? 0 : 50), previousDeadline ?? Infinity);
    if (previousDeadline !== undefined && previousDeadline <= now) { flushReadyNotifications(); return; }
    if (at >= readyNotificationAt) return;
    cancelReadyNotification(); readyNotificationAt = at;
    readyNotificationTimer = setTimeout(flushReadyNotifications, at - now);
  }
  function notify(watch: Watch) {
    readyNotifications.delete(watch);
    for (const subscriber of watch.subscribers) subscriber();
  }

  function scheduleExpiry(watch: Watch, nextExpiry: number | null, valueServer: QueryResult<unknown>["valueServer"], valueRetry?: number) {
    armReadExpiry(watch, { device: null, server: nextExpiry, localWakeup: false, valueDevice: null, valueServer, valueRetry }, clock,
      () => !closed && watches.get(watch.key) === watch, sweepExpiries, undefined, link === "online");
  }

  function sweepExpiries() {
    correctReadExpiries(watches.values(), clock, withdrawExpiry,
      (watch, d) => scheduleExpiry(watch, d.server, d.valueServer, d.valueRetry),
      watch => { if (watches.get(watch.key) === watch) void acquireRead(watch); });
  }

  function withdrawExpiry(watch: Watch): () => void {
    const deliver = watch.expire();
    return () => { deliver(); if (watches.get(watch.key) === watch) notify(watch); };
  }

  // @ref LLP 3000.018#3.2 — a server's reply decides the refresh interval; a transport failure keeps it, so it retries.
  function schedulePresence(watch: Watch, answered: boolean, after?: number) {
    // A closed client or ended credentials keep no interval, whichever read settles last.
    if (closed || !credentials) watch.presenceEvery = undefined;
    else if (answered) watch.presenceEvery = after;
    watch.presenceRefresh = presenceRefresh(watch.presenceRefresh, watch.presenceEvery, () => {
      watch.presenceRefresh = null;
      void refreshPresence(watch);
    });
  }

  // @ref LLP 3000.018#3.2 — a presence refresh reads in the background and publishes only an answer that
  // nothing replaced meanwhile: expiry, invalidation and sign-out keep their own immediate withdrawal.
  async function refreshPresence(watch: Watch) {
    if (closed || storeRefusal || !credentials || watches.get(watch.key) !== watch) return;
    if (watch.inFlight) return; // The fetch in flight re-arms the refresh when it settles.
    const shown = watch.snapshot;
    const result = await runQuery(watch.op, watch.args, true);
    if (closed || watches.get(watch.key) !== watch) return;
    // A reply that a newer publication or read overtook decides nothing: the newer one armed the timer.
    if (watch.snapshot !== shown || watch.inFlight) return;
    // A failed or stale reply keeps the answer and the interval, and retries.
    if (result.transportOffline || result.deadlineWithdrawn?.() || !currentRead(result, watch)) { schedulePresence(watch, false); return; }
    if (watch.snapshot !== shown || watch.inFlight || watches.get(watch.key) !== watch) return;
    // An accepted reply is published whatever it says, loading and refusals included.
    watch.tables = new Set(result.tables); watch.scopes = result.scopes; watch.answerGeneration = result.generation;
    watch.publish(result.read);
    acceptDelivery(result, watch.snapshot);
    ephemeralReads.incomplete(watch, "data" in result.read && !result.read.complete);
    if (!watch.kept) scheduleExpiry(watch, result.nextExpiry, result.valueServer);
    notify(watch);
    schedulePresence(watch, true, result.refresh);
  }

  const readWork = new ObservationWork<Watch>(false);
  function scheduleRead(watch: Watch, changed = false, preserved?: unknown, evidenceGeneration?: unknown) {
    if (changed && typeof evidenceGeneration === "number" && watch.answerGeneration !== undefined && evidenceGeneration !== watch.answerGeneration) watch.reset({ loading: true }, false, "program-generation");
    const bounded = changed && watchCanRevoke(watch, preserved);
    if (closed || storeRefusal) { watch.reset({ denied: storeRefusal ?? ended() }); notify(watch); return; }
    // Online change evidence may be revocation: its budget includes capacity wait.
    watch.reload(bounded, { queued: !bounded }); notify(watch);
    acquireRead(watch);
    if (changed) readWork.prioritize(watch);
  }
  function acquireRead(watch: Watch) {
    if (watch.inFlight) { watch.dirty = true; return; }
    readWork.schedule(watch, signal => refresh(watch, signal));
  }
  async function refresh(watch: Watch, signal: AbortSignal) {
    if (watches.get(watch.key) !== watch) return;
    if (closed || storeRefusal) { watch.reset({ denied: storeRefusal ?? ended() }); notify(watch); return; }
    if (watch.inFlight) {
      watch.dirty = true;
      return;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.023#3.5 — every option follows the reset fence; retention never renews an episode.
    watch.inFlight = true;
    const resetVersion = watch.resetVersion;
    let exhausted = false, retryExpired = false, batchReady = true;
        if phase(shared, engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.run_timeline_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget)), drain, "timeline delivery")?.is_none() { break; }
        chunks += 1;
    }
    Ok(chunks)
}

// @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — ordinary-priority bulk work yields
// outside every shared lock. Aged service protects finite backlogs from traffic.
const BULK_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum BulkKind { Delivery, Recount, Job }
pub(super) struct State {
    next: BulkKind,
    serviced: Instant,
    resume_at: Instant,
    empty_pause: Duration,
    advancing: bool,
    // Explicit mutation-boundary tests pause all background maintenance until drain.
    paused: bool,
    // Separate clock callers (and a dev reset) may wait here; maintenance never takes this lock.
    pub(super) advance: Arc<std::sync::Mutex<()>>,
}
impl Default for State {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let now = Instant::now();
        Self { next: BulkKind::Delivery, serviced: now, resume_at: now, empty_pause: Duration::ZERO,
            advancing: false, paused: false, advance: Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(())) }
    }
}
impl State {
    fn aged(&self, now: Instant) -> bool { now.saturating_duration_since(self.serviced) >= BULK_AGE }
    fn eligible(&self, now: Instant, aged_only: bool) -> bool {
        !self.advancing && !self.paused && if aged_only { self.aged(now) } else { now >= self.resume_at }
    }
    fn completed(&mut self, now: Instant, elapsed: Duration, contended: bool, worked: bool) {
        self.next = match self.next { BulkKind::Delivery => BulkKind::Recount, BulkKind::Recount => BulkKind::Job, BulkKind::Job => BulkKind::Delivery };
        self.serviced = now;
        // Target 1/8 CPU duty under contention, charging consumed thread CPU,
        // including atomic overruns. Cap at 100 ms; aged service is independent.
        let cap = Duration::from_millis(100);
        let charged = if contended { (elapsed * 7).min(cap) } else { Duration::ZERO };
        self.empty_pause += charged;
        // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — empty kinds keep their CPU
        // charge, but repay it with the next useful slice or the empty cycle.
        // Avoid two extra scheduler wakeups for every single delivery slice.
        let pause = if worked || self.empty_pause >= cap { self.empty_pause.min(cap) } else { Duration::ZERO };
        self.empty_pause -= pause;
        // An aged slice can finish after bulk flushes empty-cycle debt. Append
        // this charge without discarding repayment still owed in that ordering.
        self.resume_at = self.resume_at.max(now) + pause;
    }

    fn empty_cycle(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Duration {
        let pause = std::mem::take(&mut self.empty_pause);
        // A timed worker may just have scheduled repayment. Append remaining
        // debt rather than overlapping (and losing) either charge.
        self.resume_at = self.resume_at.max(now) + pause;
        self.resume_at.saturating_duration_since(now)
### 15.4 Supplied client sessions

DX6 amends the online legacy-bearer case in [LLP 3000.000.000.001 §11](./3000.000.000.001-staging-with-real-users.rfc.md#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6); complete/local validation remains as below.

Online and local constructors validate a supplied session before any transport,
device/storage work or timer. Require a non-array object with nonempty principal,
kind and token strings, a positive finite safe-integer expiresAt in milliseconds,
and valid optional scope/capExpiresAt fields. Legacy scope-less sessions remain
accepted. Expiry relative to a possibly skewed local clock is server authority;
shape validation must not reject a structurally valid past timestamp. Malformed
input throws E_SESSION with the offending field and a repair: pass the complete
Session returned by guest/login/signup or service minting, or use the existing
online token option for a bare bearer. Never include credentials in the error.
capExpiresAt without scope remains accepted legacy metadata; neither grants read authority.
Stored-session validation retains its existing auth restore path. Snapshot validated primitive fields so caller mutation cannot manufacture a NaN
timer later. Existing intentional full-session refresh and read/action expiry
semantics remain unchanged; shape validation grants no authority.

# Frontend data and actionable notes

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Review
**Systems:** snapback4-client, snapback4-language, snapback4-cli
**Author:** Charlie Cheever (implementation by Codex)
**Date:** 2026-10-18
**Revised:** 2026-10-18
**Related:** LLP 3000.013 §6, LLP 3000.031, LLP 3000.036 §17, LLP 3000.040

## 1. Evidence and boundaries

Wave 16's paired bakeoff has frontend medians 200 and 75.5 nonblank
lines. Starter production examples count as frontend even when never imported.
The report attributes all 50 pairs using the unchanged harness buckets.
FRONT owns the additive React surface and completeness presentation here.
RULES5 owns refusing-clause traces and role implications; LANG4 owns other
notes; DAY5 owns starter adaptation; KIT4 owns clock caps and test sessions;
STAGE owns provisioning. SEC owns security invariants. No change to contract.ts,
authorization, completeness certificates or protocol is proposed here.

## 2. A short common read

Wave-20 list presentation addition: LLP 3000.000.000.001 §10 defines the
packaged ReadState component; the query hooks below retain their contracts.

Add `useQueryData(op, args, options?)` in `snapback4/react`, shared by React
and Expo. It delegates directly to `useQuery` and returns `R | undefined`:
loading gives undefined; present, absent and partial give their exact data
(including null, empty lists, false and zero). A denied read throws
`QueryRefusal extends Error`, carrying the original typed `why: Refusal`.
An error boundary may render it; callers choosing inline refusal UI use
`useQueryResult`, which returns `{data: R | undefined, why: Refusal | null,
read: Read<R>}` without throwing. These hooks do not substitute an empty list for loading/refusal. They add
no cache, previous-argument retention or observation ownership. `read` is the same snapshot as `useQuery`.

A data-only read does not promise completeness or freshness. It is for
screens that show the authorized rows that arrived. Pagination, absence proofs,
and actions depending on fresh or complete results continue to use `useQuery`
or `usePage` and their metadata. The precise API remains unchanged. Skipping
uses the existing typed arguments and `{skip: true}`. Required/mistyped
arguments still fail typechecking; result types come from generated operations.
`useMutation(op).run` is already a typed callable and remains unchanged.

Add `<SignedIn fallback={...} restoring={...}>...</SignedIn>` inside
AppProvider (bare SnapbackProvider is insufficient), using the same
`useAuth()` snapshot as AppProvider. Signed-in children mount only in that
state; restoring defaults to null, signed-out uses fallback. The existing
AppProvider account boundary resets account-owned state. Children must be JSX
components (React elements), not eagerly invoked component functions.
No bundled login UI or new session runtime is added to the React entry.

## 3. Completeness notes

Default check output leads with a checked repair against the author's own
query when the binder provides one. Print each distinct repair body once,
including different query names sharing one explanation. Otherwise print the
specific missing pin or the analyzer limitation, never invent a complete form.
The next line states impact: displaying returned rows can keep the query;
proving absence or all matches needs completeness and the precise API.
The final line names any audience proved complete. Verbose keeps full evidence.
Within one invocation deduplicate only identical findings (code, site, message
and repair), preserving different scans at the same operation site. Deduplicate
identical repair bodies separately. Recheck output follows the existing dev
watch lifecycle; no persistent acknowledgement is introduced. No persistent suppression
file, diagnostic acceptance syntax, severity change or new check exit is added.

## 4. UTC hour of day

Keep `hour(t)` as the continuous epoch hour needed by bucket keys and ranges.
Add `hourOfDay(t)` for UTC clock hours 0 through 23. It requires one present
time, returns int, and equals floor(t / 1h).rem_euclid(24), including negative
times. Lower it to existing Calendar and integer arithmetic; no new wire enum
or device runtime requirement. Index/group-key use is outside this addition
(the repeating clock hour is not a chronological key). Existing offset forms
of hour remain unchanged. Guide examples distinguish both at a fixed instant.
Clock cap/session changes belong to KIT4.

## 5. Sweep-only deadline advisory

Add advisory `N_SWEEP_DEADLINE` for a scheduled Quarry job which compares
now (or its periodEnd argument) with a row's time column and directly updates a string/bool column to a literal, when a mutation reads
that table and mentions a column in require/if which either the job updates,
or both the job tests and the mutation directly updates (the §8 coupling),
but has no direct time comparison on the corresponding row deadline. This
is a conservative syntactic warning, not a proof of a vulnerability or safety.
Name job, mutation, table and deadline. Explain sweep lag and ask for an
explicit comparison of that bound row deadline with now, with the intended
opening/closing direction and equality boundary. Do not invent a guard:
opening transitions and expiry transitions have opposite directions. Do not warn on
jobs without a schedule or on mutations already comparing that row deadline.
Limit claims to direct read/loop bindings and update expressions. Upserts,
int-coded statuses, read-filter-only tests, helper-mediated and computed
transitions may be missed. Suppression recognizes direct comparisons on the
same row alias, not semantic row identity; another alias or a helper guard
can cause a false positive. Any such comparison suppresses the advisory,
without proving that it enforces a deadline. FRONT owns this initial lint;
LANG4 owns unrelated notes and can maintain subsequent lint presentation. No runtime behavior or authorization changes. Tests include
positive, guarded, unrelated-clock, unscheduled, opening, equality-boundary
and unrelated-table cases.

## 6. Starters and qualification

Stop emitting recipes/production into ordinary projects. Package the same
recipe sources as build artifacts under node_modules/snapback4/dist/recipes;
the guide copies from that location when explicitly requested by the builder.
This is explicitly assigned to FRONT by work item 1, including permission
to remove starter-shipped recipe files; it changes no DAY5 adaptation behavior
or STAGE provisioning design. Existing projects keep any previously emitted
copies. New copies come from the installed, version-pinned tarball; integration
chooses the next package version without this branch bumping it. The existing
packed production qualification executes the exact guide commands.
Native smoke remains proof support owned by DAY5 and is attributed separately.
Every changed guide snippet runs from the installed pack; additive React hooks
get type inference, refusal, empty/partial data, skip, identity and subscription
lifecycle tests. Three evidence copies are rewritten and checked with the same
line counter without editing original lanes or dropping application features.

## 7. Review and implementation record

2026-10-18: Muse round 1 and fresh Codex rounds 1–2 found incorrect sweep
repair direction and loss of same-site scan/audience evidence. The RFC and
implementation now keep every distinct finding, deduplicate exact repair bodies
and repeated context separately, qualify audiences with the scan, and never
invent the temporal guard direction. Scope/version custody, syntactic lint
limits, AppProvider and identity tests were clarified. Fresh Codex round 3
and Muse round 2 both returned READY on the revised design and implementation.
Verbatim outputs with invocation provenance are under llp/reviews/3000.013.002-*
(the initial Muse stdout includes quota failure and successful key fallback).
This remains Review until implementation qualification is recorded; no author
acceptance or publication is inferred from the technical reviews.

## 8. Wave 16 source-repro correction: confirmation versus overdue

Read-only copies of the actual schemas showed that the first §5 trigger catches
020 releaseVehicle but misses 043 confirmItem. The wedding sweep tests
`not i.confirmed and now >= i.deadline` and sets `overdue: true`; confirmation
checks `not i.confirmed` and writes `confirmed: true`, but never checks overdue.
A status-only gate lint misses exactly the reported omission.

  async function poll() {
    if (polling) return;
    polling = true;
    while (!closed && !storeRefusal && !changesRefusal && watches.size > 0) {
      if (link !== "online") {
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
        if (!closed && credentials && link === "offline") await probe();
        continue;
      }
      if (!backendLoaded && ephemeralAdvertised && !await loadBackend()) continue;
      const eph = ephemeralReads.pollRequest();
      try {
        if (refreshing) await refreshing;
        if (closed || storeRefusal || !credentials) break;
        const revision = credentialRevision;
        const floor = minimumReadSeq;
        const { response, json } = await changes(seq, options.waitSeconds ?? 25, { ...headers }, storeIdentity, hasEphemeral() ? ephemeralReads.parameters() : undefined, eph.signal);
        if (closed || storeRefusal) break;
        if (!ephemeralReads.current(eph)) continue;
        observeStore(json as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, floor, false, seq <= minimumReadSeq ? (json.generation === queryBackend?.generation ? intervalChanges(json.changes, seq, json.seq) : undefined) ?? json.tables : undefined);
        if (storeRefusal) break;
        if (await obsoleteRefusal(json.denied, revision)) continue;
        if (closed || storeRefusal || !credentials) break;
        if (await pauseRefusal(response, json.denied, aborter.signal)) continue;
        if (refusal(json.denied)) {
          changesRefusal = json.denied; queryEpoch++; pendingEvents.notify();
          const deliver = deferReadNotifications(() => { for (const watch of watches.values()) { watch.reset({ denied: changesRefusal! }); scheduleExpiry(watch, null, null); } });
          deliver(); for (const watch of watches.values()) notify(watch);
          break;
        }
        if (!ephemeralReads.changed(eph, json.ephemeral)) continue;
        if (!response.ok || json.denied) throw new Error(`changes: HTTP ${response.status}`);
        const changedGeneration = generation !== undefined && json.generation !== undefined && generation !== json.generation;
        minimumReadSeq = Math.max(minimumReadSeq, json.seq);
        if (changedGeneration) {
          const deliver = resetWatches(false, "program-generation");
          queryEpoch++;
          deliver();
          for (const watch of watches.values()) notify(watch);
          if ((backend || ephemeralAdvertised) && !await loadBackend(json.generation)) continue;
        }
        generation = backend?.generation ?? json.generation;
        if (json.seq > seq || changedGeneration) {
          seq = Math.max(seq, json.seq);
          for (const watch of watches.values()) {
            if (changedGeneration || evidenceTouches(watch, json.tables)) void scheduleRead(watch, true, json.preserved, json.generation);
          }
        }
      } catch (error) {
        transportFailure = transportRefusal(error);
        if (closed || storeRefusal) break;
        if (!ephemeralReads.current(eph)) continue;
        if (credentials) setLink("offline");
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
      }
    }
    polling = false;
  }

  async function hold(entry: (typeof pending)[number], reason: string) {
    entry.state = "held"; entry.reason = reason;
    pendingEvents.notify();
    await dispatch.owner?.held?.(entry.id, reason);
  }
  async function send(entry: (typeof pending)[number]): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = await post(`/m/${entry.op.name}`, { id: entry.id, args: entry.args }, entry.response);
    if (storeRefusal) return true;
    if (!result.ok) return false;
                // on the next asset tick; do not spin on skipped slices.
                if timely_engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| engine.finish_asset_sweep_for(&mut sweep, budget))? == 0 { break; }
            }
            Ok(())
        })(), false, "the asset sweep")?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn maintain(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
    let mut tick = 0;
    let mut last_tick = Instant::now();
    loop {
        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
        {
            let state = lock(&shared.maintenance);
            if !state.advancing && !state.paused && let Err(error) = shared.ephemeral.sweep(shared.now()) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: ephemeral sweep: {error}"); }
        }
        if last_tick.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(1) {
            tick += 1; last_tick = Instant::now();
            // One bounded reclamation slice; abandoned bytes remain charged
            // until their actual records are dropped. No engine lock is held.
            lock(&shared.following).reclaim(shared.now(), snapback4_core::Ceilings { heap_bytes: 1 << 20, ..Default::default() });
            if let Err(error) = timely(&shared, tick) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: maintenance failed: {error}"); }
        }
        promote_aged(&shared, Instant::now());
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
    }
}

fn promote_aged(shared: &Shared, now: Instant) {
    if lock(&shared.maintenance).eligible(now, true) { bulk_slice(shared, true); }
}

pub(super) fn bulk(shared: Arc<Shared>) {
    loop {
        if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
        let observed = *lock(&shared.seq);
        let mut did_work = false;
        for _ in 0..3 {
            if crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return; }
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — a browser proof calls the app's own authenticated client.
import type { App } from "./app.js";
import type { Mutation, Write } from "./contract.js";

export type WitnessReason = "not-ready" | "returned-early" | "event" | "arguments";

type Operations = Record<string, unknown>;
export function registerWebWitness(app: App, api?: Operations): void {
  const handlerClaim = Symbol("witness explicit handler claim");
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.location?.search !== "string") return;
  const locked = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window, "__snapback4Witness");
  if (locked?.configurable === false) {
    (locked.value as { registerApi(owner: App, api?: Operations): void }).registerApi(app, api);
    return;
  }
  const enabled = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("witness") === "web";
  const armed = () => { if (!enabled) throw Object.assign(Error("E_WITNESS_DISABLED: add &witness=web"), { code: "E_WITNESS_DISABLED" }); };
  const operations: unknown[] = [];
  const walk = (value: Operations) => { for (const entry of Object.values(value)) {
    if (entry && typeof entry === "object") {
      if ("kind" in entry && entry.kind === "mutation" && !("ephemeral" in entry && entry.ephemeral === true)) operations.push(entry);
      else if (!("kind" in entry)) walk(entry as Operations);
    }
  } };
  if (api) walk(api);
  let ready = false;
  void app.ready.then(result => { ready = result.ok; }, () => {});
  let form: { terminal: Promise<unknown>; cancel: () => void; claimHandler: (owner: App, event: Event) => { mutate: App["mutate"]; done: () => void } | undefined } | undefined;
  Object.defineProperty(window, "__snapback4Witness", { configurable: true, value: {
    registerApi: (owner: App, operationsApi?: Operations) => {
      if (owner !== app) throw Error("E_WITNESS_APP_SELECTION: this proof selected another App; use a separate page for another application instance");
      if (operationsApi) { api = operationsApi; operations.length = 0; walk(operationsApi); }
    },
    ready: () => app.ready,
    viewer: () => app.client?.viewer(),
    clock: () => {
      armed();
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: no signed-in client clock");
      return app.client.now();
    },
    beginForm: (name: string | undefined, expected: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined, button: Element) => {
      armed();
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: no signed-in client");
      form?.cancel();
      const client = app.client, originalClient = client.mutate, originalApp = app.mutate;
      let click: Event | undefined, submit: Event | undefined, clickTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined, claimed = false, cancelObserver = () => {};
      let clickedReady = false, handlerClaimed = false;
      const handlerToken = Symbol("witness handler claim");
      const outsideClick = Symbol("witness outside clicked dispatch");
      let resolve!: (write: unknown) => void;
      const terminal = new Promise(answer => { resolve = answer; });
      let settled = false, noWriteTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      const noWrite = () => finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_NO_WRITE", reason: clickedReady ? "returned-early" : "not-ready", message: "armed click produced no durable mutation (form handler returned early, or the app was not ready)" } });
      const finish = (write: unknown) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true; clearTimeout(expiry); clearTimeout(noWriteTimer); restore(); cancelObserver(); resolve(write);
      };
      // Bound even an abandoned arm or a claimed write that never settles.
      const receiptExpired = () => finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_SENT", message: "claimed write did not settle within 30000 ms; inspect its original intent id" } });
      let expiry = setTimeout(noWrite, 30_000);
      // Chromium's active event ties admission to this actual control's handler.
      // Timers, input autosaves and previously-started writes cannot claim it.
      const capture = (event: Event) => {
        if (handlerClaimed) return; // A later click cannot restart the held handler timer.
        if (!event.isTrusted) { click = undefined; submit = undefined; clearTimeout(clickTimer); return; }
        if (event.isTrusted && event.composedPath().includes(button)) {
          click = event; submit = undefined; clickedReady = ready;
          clearTimeout(noWriteTimer);
          noWriteTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); }, 1000);
          clearTimeout(clickTimer);
          clickTimer = setTimeout(() => { click = undefined; submit = undefined; }, 0);
        }
      };
      const captureSubmit = (event: Event) => {
        if (click && event.isTrusted && (event as SubmitEvent).submitter === button) submit = event;
      };
      document.addEventListener("click", capture, true);
      document.addEventListener("submit", captureSubmit, true);
      const restore = () => {
        if (client.mutate === clientWrapper) client.mutate = originalClient;
        if (app.mutate === appWrapper) app.mutate = originalApp;
        document.removeEventListener("click", capture, true);
        document.removeEventListener("submit", captureSubmit, true); clearTimeout(clickTimer);
      };
      const canonical = (value: unknown): unknown => Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(canonical) : value && typeof value === "object"
        ? Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(value).sort().map(key => [key, canonical((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key])])) : value;
      const execute = async (original: Function, receiver: unknown, op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any): Promise<Write> => {
        const durable = api ? operations.some(value => (value as Mutation<any, any>).name === op.name) : op.kind === "mutation" && (op as { ephemeral?: boolean }).ephemeral !== true;
        const eventMatches = options?.[handlerClaim] !== undefined
          ? handlerClaimed && options[handlerClaim] === handlerToken
          : !!click && (window.event === click || (!!submit && window.event === submit));
        const argsMatch = expected == null || JSON.stringify(canonical(expected)) === JSON.stringify(canonical(args));
        const eligible = !claimed && eventMatches && !options?.[outsideClick] && durable && (!name || name === op.name) && argsMatch;
        let forwarded = options;
        if (!eligible && receiver === app && durable) {
          const { id, waitSeconds, ...other } = options ?? {};
          forwarded = { ...other, id, waitSeconds, [outsideClick]: true };
        }
        const invoke = () => original.call(receiver, op, args, forwarded) as Promise<Write>;
        if (!eligible) {
          if (!claimed && durable) {
            claimed = true; restore();
            const reason = !eventMatches || options?.[outsideClick] ? "event: expected the armed click or its synchronous submit"
              : name && name !== op.name ? `operation: expected ${name}, got ${op.name}`
              : `arguments: mismatched keys: ${[...new Set([...Object.keys(expected ?? {}), ...Object.keys(args)])].sort().filter(key => JSON.stringify(canonical(expected?.[key])) !== JSON.stringify(canonical(args[key]))).join(", ")}`;
            if (eventMatches && !options?.[outsideClick] && (!name || name === op.name) && !argsMatch) {
              const diagnostic = (window as unknown as { __snapback4ProofDiagnostic?: (value: unknown) => Promise<void> }).__snapback4ProofDiagnostic;
              // The proof driver writes values only to its mode-0600 private log.
              // A failed diagnostic must never prevent the application's write.
              try { void diagnostic?.({ code: "E_WITNESS_UNCLAIMED", mutation: op.name, armed: expected, actual: args }).catch(() => {}); } catch { /* Diagnostic failure cannot block the write. */ }
            }
            finish({ state: "failed", mutation: op.name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_UNCLAIMED", reason: !eventMatches || options?.[outsideClick] ? "event" : "arguments", message: reason } });
          }
          return invoke();
        }
        claimed = true; restore(); clearTimeout(expiry); clearTimeout(noWriteTimer);
        // A late click receives its own complete settlement allowance.
        expiry = setTimeout(receiptExpired, 30_000);
        let id: string | undefined, early: Write[] = [];
        const stop = client.onWrite(write => {
          // Native clients publish terminal receipts; foreign adapters must not
          // turn a pending notification into terminal proof evidence either.
          if ((write as Write).state === "pending") return;
          if (id === undefined) { early.push(write); if (early.length > 256) early.shift(); }
          else if (write.id === id) { stop(); finish({ ...write, mutation: op.name }); }
        });
        cancelObserver = stop;
        try {
          const write = await invoke();
          id = write.id;
          const receipt = write.state === "pending" ? early.find(value => value.id === id && value.state !== "pending") : write;
          early = [];
          if (receipt) { stop(); finish({ ...receipt, mutation: op.name }); }
          return write;
        } catch (error) { stop(); finish({ state: "failed", why: { code: "E_WITNESS_WEB" } }); throw error; }
      };
      // Hooks submit through client; forms using app.mutate enter before its readiness await.
      const clientWrapper = ((op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any) => execute(originalClient, client, op, args, options)) as typeof client.mutate;
      const appWrapper = ((op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any) => execute(originalApp, app, op, args, options)) as typeof app.mutate;
      client.mutate = clientWrapper; app.mutate = appWrapper;

      const claimHandler = (owner: App, event: Event) => {
        if (owner !== app || handlerClaimed || claimed || settled || !event.isTrusted
          || !(click && event === click && window.event === click || submit && event === submit && window.event === submit)) return undefined;
        handlerClaimed = true; clearTimeout(noWriteTimer);
        return {
          mutate: ((op: Mutation<any, any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, options?: any) =>
            app.mutate(op, args, { ...options, [handlerClaim]: handlerToken })) as App["mutate"],
          done: () => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); },
        };
      };
      form = { terminal, claimHandler, cancel: () => { if (!claimed) noWrite(); else finish({ state: "failed", mutation: name, why: { code: "E_WITNESS_UNCLAIMED", reason: "event", message: "rearmed before the claimed write settled; inspect its original intent id" } }); } };
    },
    claimHandler: (owner: App, event: Event) => form?.claimHandler(owner, event),
    formResult: () => form?.terminal,
    mutate: async (name: string | undefined, args: Record<string, unknown>, id: string) => {
      armed();
      const operation = operations.find(value => !name || (value as { name: string }).name === name);
      if (!operation) throw Error("E_WITNESS_MUTATION: no matching durable mutation; pass --mutation with a generated API name (ephemeral signals do not prove stored writes)");
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: the app has no signed-in client");
      let observed: unknown;
      let settle: ((write: unknown) => void) | undefined;
      const stop = app.client.onWrite(write => { if (write.id === id) { observed = write; settle?.(write); } });
      try {
        const write = await app.mutate(operation as Mutation<any, any>, args, { id, waitSeconds: 0 });
        if (write.state !== "pending") return write;
        return observed ?? await new Promise(resolve => { settle = resolve; });
      } finally { stop(); }
    },
  } });
}

/** @internal Shared by the Node proof kit and the native browser driver. */
export function webWitnessObserverScript(): string {
  return `(() => {
    const register = (${registerWebWitness.toString()});
    let selected;
    Object.defineProperty(window, Symbol.for("snapback4.proof.app"), { configurable: false, writable: false, value: app => {
      if (selected) return;
      selected = app;
      register(app);
      const bridge = window.__snapback4Witness;
      Object.freeze(bridge);
      Object.defineProperty(window, "__snapback4Witness", { configurable: false, writable: false, value: bridge });
    }});
  })();`;
}

/** Run an event handler with an explicit write claim that survives awaited uploads.
 * @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11 — only explicit per-arm custody survives an async handler.
 * Call synchronously from the clicked control. Use the supplied mutate inside
 * the callback; it carries only that arm's token, never another handler's writes.
 */
export async function witnessForm<T>(app: App, event: Event | { nativeEvent: Event }, handler: (mutate: App["mutate"]) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const native = "nativeEvent" in event ? event.nativeEvent : event;
  const bridge = typeof window === "undefined" ? undefined : (window as unknown as { __snapback4Witness?: {
    claimHandler(owner: App, event: Event): { mutate: App["mutate"]; done(): void } | undefined;
  } }).__snapback4Witness;
  const claim = bridge?.claimHandler(app, native);
  try { return await handler(claim?.mutate ?? app.mutate.bind(app)); }
  finally { claim?.done(); }
}
